金利上昇による企業の負担増加は 
いつ本格化するか

○ 日銀の政策金利や、金融市場で決まる国債金利に比べ、企業が実際に負担する金利の上昇はまだ
限定的。企業の新規借入金利や負債残高全体への影響波及に時間がかかることが背景 
○ 金融機関の新規貸出金利は、概ね3カ月かけて政策金利の変化を反映する傾向。特に地域金融機関
で上昇がやや遅く、企業規模により有利子負債利子率の上昇ペースが異なる一因に 
○ 借入・社債金利の動きを踏まえると、企業の利払い負担増加は2026～2028年度に本格化し、年間
約3兆円の利益下押し要因に。これまで以上に資本効率を意識した経営が求められる

１．政策金利や国債金利が上昇するも、企業の利払い負担増加はまだ本格化せず 
日本銀行は2026年9月の金融政策決定会合で、 政策金利を1.0％から1.25％へ引き上げた。これは1995
年以来、31年ぶりの高水準である1。また、金融市場で決まる10年国債金利（いわゆる長期金利）は、
2026年9月に節目となる3％を超えた。こちらも1996年以来30年ぶりの高さであり、政策金利や国債金
利はまさに歴史的な転換点を迎えている。 
一方で、 企業が実際に負担する金利の上昇は今のところ限定的だ。 図表1は、 企業の有利子負債利子
率 （＝支払利息等÷有利子負債残高） を指標金利 （政策金利、10年国債金利）と比較したものである。

### Page 2

図中の凡例にある数字は、 各金利についてマイナス金利政策解除 （2024年3月19日 ）前 の2023年10～12
月期と、直近の2026年4～6月期を比較した値だ。政策金利が＋0.93％Pt、10年国債金利が＋1.80％Pt
と大きく上昇した一方で、企業の有利子負債利子率は＋0.42％Ptと上昇幅が小さく、企業の利払い負
担の増加はまだ本格化していない。実際、日銀短観における企業の資金繰りに関する調査（図表2）を
みると、「楽である」の回答率から「苦しい」の回答率を差し引いたDIが、2021年以降プラス圏で概
ね横ばいを維持している。企業の実感としても、これまでに実施された複数回の利上げや、金融市場
における国債金利の大幅な上昇は、まだ資金調達環境の悪化につながっていないようだ。 
なぜ、こうしたギャップが生じるのか。主な要因として考えられるのは次の2点である。 
第一の要因は、指標金利と企業の新規調達金利とのタイムラグだ。政策金利などの指標金利が変化
してから、企業が新たに資金を調達する際の金利が変化するまで、少し時間がかかる。特に、企業の
有利子負債の半分強を占める金融機関からの借入において（図表3）、新規調達金利のタイムラグが生
じやすいと考えられる。 
第二の要因は、企業の新規調達金利と有利子負債残高全体の利払い負担とのタイムラグだ。企業が
新たに資金調達する際の金利が上昇しても、それが有利子負債残高全体にすぐ反映されるわけではな
い。 企業の金融機関借入金の4～5割程度が固定金利型であるほか2、 主に大企業が発行する社債の多く
が固定利付債であり、過去の低金利時代に調達した負債がまだ残っているためだ。 
本稿では、 これら2つの要因を踏まえて、政策金利・国債金利などの指標金利上昇を受けた企業の利
払い負担増加が、いつ本格的に現れるかを試算する3。 
２．金融機関からの新規借入金利は、地域金融機関で上昇ペースが遅い傾向 
まず、第一の要因（指標金利から企業の新規調達金利へのタイムラグ）の影響を検証しよう。図表
3に示したように、 企業の有利子負債には金融機関などからの借入金と、 主に大企業が発行する社債が
ある。このうち、社債を新規発行する際の金利は概ね国債金利に連動して決まり、タイムラグがほぼ
図表 3 企業の有利子負債残高の内訳 図表 4 金融機関業態別の新規貸出金利

含まれるが、 シェアが小さい （0.1％程度） ため表示し

無いため、ここでの検証は金融機関などから新規に借り入れる際の金利が対象になる。 
図表4は、金融機関の新規貸出金利（金融機関からみた貸出）を、業態別に示したものだ。都市銀行
では新規貸出金利の上昇ペースが速い一方、第二地方銀行や信用金庫では政策金利の上昇に比べて新
規貸出金利の上昇ペースがやや遅い。こうした地域金融機関における金利上昇の遅れが、指標金利と
企業の新規調達金利のタイムラグに影響していると考えられる。 
金融機関の業態によって新規貸出金利の上昇ペースに差があるのは、貸出の金利形態別シェアが異
なることが一因であろう。大手行では企業向け貸出全体の約6割を占める変動金利型貸出のうち、ほと
んどが「市場金利連動」であるため、金融市場の変化による影響がすぐに反映されやすい。他方で、
地方銀行や信用金庫では変動金利型貸出のうち「短期プライムレート連動」のシェアが高い。短期プ
ライムレートとは、各金融機関が設定する企業向け貸出の最優遇金利である。政策金利の変更などを
反映して決められることが多いが、各金融機関の競争環境などによって反映時期・度合いに差が生じ
る4。これにより、第二地方銀行や信用金庫では都市銀行に比べて新規貸出金利の上昇ペースが遅くな
る傾向があると考えられる5。 
こうした金融機関業態別の違いは、企業サイドにおいて、有利子負債利子率の上昇ペースが規模別
に異なる一因になっている可能性がある。足元の金利上昇局面では、都市銀行からの借入が多いと考
えられる大企業（資本金10億円以上）で有利子負債利子率の上昇ペースが速く、信用金庫など地域金
融機関からの借入が多いと考えられる小規模企業（同1千万円未満）で上昇ペースが遅い（図表5）。
2010年代の金利低下局面では大企業の有利子負債利子率が小規模企業を下回る傾向にあったが、足元
では完全に逆転している。大企業は社債が有利子負債残高の約3分の1を占めるため、金融機関の新規
貸出金利のみが原因とは言えないものの、金融機関業態別の違いが企業規模別の資金調達環境に影響
を及ぼしている可能性がある点は興味深い。 
では、今後日銀がさらに利上げを進めていくと、金融機関の新規貸出金利はどの程度のペースで、
どこまで上昇していくか。過去の政策金利と新規貸出金利の関係を統計的に分析すると、 政策金利1％

平均値で除した値、対象は金融・保険を除く全産業

### Page 4

4

Ptの上昇は新規貸出金利を当月に0.31％Pt押し上げ、その後さらに3カ月間かけて当月からの累計で
0.85％Pt押し上げる傾向があることが明らかになった。日銀が今後2027 年半ばにかけて政策金利を
2.0％まで引き上げていくことを前提にすると6、金融機関の新規貸出金利（2026年8月時点で1.78％）
は2027年秋ごろにかけて2.65％程度へ上昇すると試算される （図表6） 。 マイナス金利政策の解除前と
比較すると、2％Pt弱の上昇になる計算だ。 
３．企業の利払い負担増加は今後 2028 年度にかけて本格化。 利益を年間約 3 兆円下押し 
次に、第二の要因（企業の新規調達金利と有利子負債残高全体の利払い負担とのタイムラグ）の影
響を検証しよう。金融機関の新規貸出金利や社債の新規発行金利の上昇が、それぞれの残高全体にど
の程度のペースで反映されるかを確認し、企業の利払い負担がいつ本格化に増加するか試算する。 
金融機関の新規貸出金利と貸出残高全体の金利を比較すると、今回の金利上昇局面では新規貸出金
の上昇に対して貸出金利残高全体の金利上昇がやや遅れている（図表7）。 先に述べたように、金融機
関の貸出の一部が固定金利型であり、新規貸出や借り換えに伴って新規貸出金利の上昇が残高全体へ
波及するのに時間がかかるためだ。 
両者の過去の関係を統計的に分析すると、新規貸出金利の上昇がおよそ5年程度かけて残高全体の金
利を緩やかに押し上げる傾向があるとの結果になった。 図表6で試算したように、 先行きの新規貸出金
利が2027年秋ごろにかけて2.65％程度まで上昇していくと想定すると、貸出残高全体の金利（2026年
8月時点で1.51％ ）は2027年末に2.0％程度、2028年末に2.3％程度と緩やかに上昇し、2030年代前半に
は新規貸出金利と同じ2.65％程度へ収束していく計算になる。 
同様に社債について、 新発債金利と既発債金利を比較したものが図表8だ。 新発債金利は2022年ごろ
から上昇して足元では2％台後半に達しているが、既発債金利はまだ1％強にとどまっており、金融機
関の貸出金利に比べると残高全体への反映に時間がかかりやすいことがうかがえる。なお、日本で新

### Page 5

5

規に発行される社債の平均年限は現在5～6年であり、新発債金利は概ね5 年国債金利に連動して動く
傾向があることから、先に述べたように指標金利→新発債金利のタイムラグはほとんど生じない。 
今後、社債残高が物価上昇にあわせて年間＋2％のペースで増加していくとの前提を置き、 先行きの
償還に伴う借り換えや追加的な新規発行が行われていく場合、既発債金利は2030年代初めにかけて年
間＋0.3％Pt程度の緩やかなペースで上昇していくと試算される（新発債金利は5年国債金利の先行き
予測値に連動すると想定）。7年債や10年債といった年限が長めの社債も一定の規模があることから、
新発債金利の上昇が既発債金利に反映されるまでに時間を要する結果になった。 
最後に、金融機関の貸出金利（企業からみた借入金利7）と社債金利の試算結果を用いて、企業の有
利子負債利回りへの影響を検証する。企業の有利子負債に占める借入と社債の金額シェアを先行き一
定として、 貸出金利と社債金利の変化を有利子負債利子率に反映したものが図表9だ。2023～2025年度
の有利子負債利子率の上昇ペースは年間＋0.1～＋0.2％Pt程度と緩やかだったが、2026～2028年度に
は同＋0.3～＋0.4％Ptに加速すると見込まれる。企業の有利子負債の多くを占める借入金の金利上昇
が、主な押し上げ要因だ。2029年以後は借入金の金利上昇が落ち着く一方、社債金利の上昇継続を受
け、有利子負債利子率は2030年代にかけて緩やかに3％程度の水準まで上昇していくだろう。 
このように、金利上昇による企業の利払い負担増加は今後2028年度にかけて本格化していく見込み
だ。企業全体の有利子負債残高が先行き年間＋2％のペースで拡大していくと仮定すると、 支払利息等
が年間約3兆円増加する計算であり、経常利益（金融・保険を除くベースで2025年度：124.8兆円）に
対し年間2.4％の押し下げ要因に相当する8。 
今後、有利子負債利子率が上昇していく過程では、それに見合った利益率を達成できるかが問われ
るだろう。2025年度時点では投下資本利益率（ROIC、3.70％）の有利子負債利子率（1.45％）に対す
るスプレッドが2％Ptを上回っており、ROICでみた企業の資本効率は外部資金調達コストを十分カバー
できている（図表10）9。しかし、 有利子負債利子率が今後3％程度へ上昇していくと、ROICが現在の水
準のままではスプレッドがコロナ禍初期の2020年度並みに縮小し、企業の利払い負担に対する利益の
図表 9 企業の有利子負債利子率の試算 図表 10 有利子負債利子率と投下資本利益率

（注） 有利子負債利子率の対象は、金融 ・ 保険を除く全産業 ・
全規模。先行きは、法人企業統計調査における借入金
残高と社債残高のシェアを一定として、有利子負債利
子率への影響を試算

6

余力が弱まってしまう。日本の金利上昇が一段と進み、企業の利払い負担が本格的に増加していくと
見込まれるなか、これまで以上に資本効率を意識した経営が求められるといえそうだ。

［参考文献］ 
みずほ総合研究所調査部（2026）「2026年・2027年 みずほ世界経済見通し ～転換点は近いのか、 投資
主導経済の行方を探る～」、2026年10月2日

1 現在の政策金利は無担保コール翌日物金利の誘導目標だが、1995 年時点では公定歩合を政策金利と見なして時系列比較してお
り、厳密には政策金利の意味するものが異なる。 
2 日本銀行「金融システムレポート」 （2026年4 月）の図表Ⅲ-1-12 によれば、法人向け貸出のうち、大手行で約40％、地域銀
行（地方銀行、第二地方銀行）や信用金庫で 45～50％程度が固定金利貸出である（2025 年 9 月末時点）。 
3 金利上昇による企業への影響には、利払いの増加によるデメリットだけでなく、利子収入の増加によるメリットもある。本稿
では、そのうちデメリットの面だけに焦点を当てて分析しており、メリットについては稿を改めて論じたい。 
4 日本銀行「金融システムレポート」 （2025年4 月）の図表Ⅵ-1-8 では、地域銀行を対象に、2024 年7 月の政策金利上昇を受け
た短期プライムレートの引き上げタイミング、引き上げ幅、引き上げ後金利の貸出先適用割合について分析が行われている。 
5 図表 4 に示した金融機関業態別の新規貸出金利には、企業向け以外の貸出も含まれる。したがって、企業向け貸出における
「市場金利連動」 、 「短期プライムレート連動」のシェアが業態別に異なることだけでなく、貸出全体に占める住宅ローン貸出
のシェアが業態別に異なることなども、新規貸出金利の上昇ペースの違いに影響していると考えられる。 
6 みずほ総合研究所調査部（2026）の見通しに基づく想定。 
7 金融機関の貸出金利には企業向け以外の貸出も含まれるが、ここでは簡易的に、企業向け貸出金利が貸出全体の金利に連動す
ると仮定して試算した。 
8 本稿では金利上昇による利払いの増加のみを計算対象としているため、利子収入の増加も考慮すれば経常利益への下押し圧力
は緩和すると考えられる。 
9 投下資本利益率（ROIC）と本来比較すべきは、負債だけでなく自己資本のコストも考慮した加重平均資本コスト（WACC）だ
が、本稿では金利上昇の影響を直接評価する観点から、投下資本利益率と有利子負債利子率を比べている。

本面，2026 年7 月9 日

如何看待PPI-CPI 剪刀差走扩？ 
        ——2026 年9 月价格数据点评

事件：2026 年 9 月全国居民消费价格指数（CPI）同比 0.9%（前
值 0.8%），环比 0.2%（前值 0.4%），1-9 月 CPI 同比 0.9%（前值
0.9%）；核心 CPI 同比 1%（前值 1%），环比0（前值 0.1%），1-9 月
核心 CPI 同比 1.1%（前值 1.1%）；工业生产者出厂价格指数（PPI）
同比5%（前值3.8%），环比1.2%（前值0.4%），1-9 月PPI 同比2.3%
（前值2%）。 
受油价、芯片等涨价影响，CPI 环比连续两个月为正,9 月略高于
季节性水平，CPI 同比涨幅较上月小幅扩大；1-9 月，CPI 同比及核心
CPI 同比均较上年底 回升但仍低于上半年水平 ，价格持续修复 但修复
节奏放缓。9 月 CPI 同比增长 0.9%，较上月回升 0.1 个百分点，连续
两个月回升，环比延续正增长， 为 0.2%，强于季节性水平（2023 年-
2025 年，9 月 CPI 环比均值为 0.1%）。9 月核心 CPI（剔除食品和能
源）同比增长 1%，与上月持平，仍位于较低水平， 环比 0，持平季节
性水平（2023 年-2025 年，9 月核心CPI 环比均值为0）。1-9 月，CPI
同比及核心 CPI 同比分别为 0.9%和 1.1%，均与 1-8 月持平，较上年底
和上年同期水平回升，但均较上半年回落 0.1 个百分点，仍位于较低
区间水平，价格修复的基础有待进一步夯实。 
 油价仍是 CPI 环比维持正增的重要支撑， 同时受成本走高影响， 本
月移动电话机、计算机、数据存储设备和平板电脑 价格环比上涨、
对CPI 环比维持正增带来一定助力。工业消费品中，受国际市场价格
变动影响，9 月国内汽油价格环比上涨4.9%，连续两个月环比正增，
油价仍是CPI 环比维持正增的重要支撑。受芯片等成本上涨影响，从
7 月开始，多家手机厂商已经陆续上调部分在售产品价格。9 月 1 日，

如何看待PPI-CPI 剪刀差走扩？ 
华为、小米、荣耀多款手机官方调整售价，单次涨幅从 200 元至 1000 元不等。9 月移动电话
机、计算机、数据存储设备和平板电脑价格环比分别上涨 3.7%、2.0%、1.7%和 1.5%，合计影
响 CPI 环比上涨约 0.04 个百分点。此外，秋装上市，带动服装价格环比上涨 0.7%，影响 CPI
环比上涨约0.03 个百分点。 
 暑期效应消退，服务价格环比由涨转降，持平季节性水平，对 CPI 环比的影响由上月支撑转
为拖累，其中飞机票、宾馆住宿等 假期出行相关服务价格环比均由正转负。 9 月，服务价格
同比上涨0.8%，涨幅与上月相同，服务价格环比由上月上涨0.1%转为下降0.2%，持平季节性
（2023-2025 年9 月，服务价格环比均值为-0.2%），对CPI 环比的影响由上月上拉约0.04 个
百分点转为下拉约 0.08 个百分点。具体来看，随着暑假结束，飞机票、宾馆住宿环比均由正
转负，飞机票、宾馆住宿和旅行社收费价格环比分别下降 8.7%、7.0%和 6.0%，合计影响 CPI
环比下降约0.12 个百分点。 
 食品价格环比维持正增长 ，但低于季节性水平 ，同比降幅边际扩大，食品价格仍偏弱 。9 月
食品价格同比下降 1.7%，降幅比上月扩大 0.3 个百分点，影响 CPI 同比下降约 0.29 个百分
点，环比上涨0.4%，连续两个月正增长，影响CPI 环比上涨约0.06 个百分点，但仍低于季节
性水平（2023-2025 年 9 月，食品价格环比均值为 0.6%）。其中，受猪肉供给结构调整以及
暑假、中秋和国庆备货需求带动，鲜菜及猪肉价格环比连续 3 个月为正。此外，休渔期结束，
水产品价格环比连续两个月为负。

如何看待PPI-CPI 剪刀差走扩？ 
品价格上行影响，石油开采、精炼石油产品制造、有机化学原料制造价格环比分别上涨14.5%、
7.4%和5.4%，有色金属矿采选业、有色金属冶炼和压延加工业价格环比分别上涨1.6%和1.5%，
5 个行业合计影响PPI 环比上涨约0.63 个百分点，是本月PPI 环比改善最重要的支撑。同时，
受冬季供暖备煤需求增加、主产矿区供应阶段性偏紧 等因素影响，煤炭开采和洗选业、煤炭
加工价格环比分别上涨9.5%和13.6%。9 月是建筑施工旺季，钢材、水泥等建筑材料需求有所
回升，加之 煤炭等原材料成本上涨，非金属矿物制品业 （主要包括水泥、玻璃等） 价格环比
由负转正，上涨0.3%，黑色金属冶炼和压延加工业价格环比上涨0.2%，4 个行业合计影响PPI
环比上涨约 0.38 个百分点。此外，人工智能、算电协同等领域加快发展，电气机械和器材制
造业、计算机通信和其他电子设备制造业等 AI 产业链相关行业价格环比均上涨 0.4%，亦对
PPI 上行带来支撑。  
 生活资料价格环比连续两个月为正但仍位于低位区间，同比降幅收窄至 0 附近。生活资料价
格同比降幅连续 3 个月收窄但仍为负，9 月同比下降 0.1%，环比延续 8 月的正增长态势、连
续两个月为正，不过仍位于0 左右的低位区间，终端需求仍偏弱。

www.ccxi.com.cn 
2026 年9 月价格数据点评  
如何看待PPI-CPI 剪刀差走扩？

整体来看，今年以来物价整体呈现温和修复态势，呈现以下特征：一是上下游景气度存
在温差，中下游尤其下游中小企业仍在承受成本挤压 。今年以来，生产资料与生活资料同比
分化走势逐渐加大，二者剪刀差由年初 1 月的 0.4 个百分点扩大到 9 月的 6.6 个百分点。结
合 PMI 数据来看，截至 9 月，主要原材料购进价格分项与出厂价格剪刀差连续两个月扩大。
涨价能力和利润持续向上游资源行业集中，终端消费品价格整体仍偏弱，中下游制造业面临
成本挤压。二是 AI 产业链涨价正逐步成为影响物价走势的新增变量。从电气机械、算力设
施等上游环节，到存储器件、平板终端、手机等下游消费品，AI 相关产品价格表现均较好，
成为价格修复的重要力量。三是当前经济仍面临供给端涨价与需求端疲软并存的矛盾，PPI-
CPI 剪刀差扩大。自今年 4 月 PPI 同比首次超过 CPI 同比以来，PPI-CPI 正剪刀差由 4 月的
1.6%波动扩大至 9 月的 4.1%，9 月较 8 月扩大 1.1 个百分点。供需失衡的格局仍在延续，价
格修复仍受到掣肘。 
后续来看，9 月以来中东局势再度升级，国际原油价格波动上行，截至10 月13 日，WTI
原油期货结算价由 9 月底的 90.42 美元/桶，波动上行至 94.1 美元/桶。若油价持续高位运
行，短期石化产业链价格仍将位于较高水平， PPI 同比保持韧性仍有支撑，并沿产业链向
CPI 能源分项传导。但也要看到，当前生产端价格改善仍主要集中于能源、有色和部分新动
能行业，终端需求相关行业价格仍偏弱，核心CPI 始终在1%左右的较低水平徘徊，全面物价
上涨的可能性较低，完成2%的年度通胀目标难度仍较大。

ab 15 October 2026
Global Research
First Read
Genius Electronic Optical
Private placement with 3M expands its optical
connectivity opportunity
Genius approved a private placement with 3M
On 15th Oct, Genius's board approved a private placement to 3M UK Limited for
5.54mn shares at NT$869.6 per share, an 18.3% discount to the 14th Oct closing price
NT$1,065, representing NT$4.8bn investment and 4.68% stake in Genius. Proceeds
from the transaction will fund technology development, working capital, and bank loan

repayment. The two companies also announced a strategic partnership to develop
optical communication for AI data center and HPC. We believe this announcement is a
positive validation for Genius working with a tier one supplier in optical connectivity to
provide its precision components for advanced optics.
3M EBO technology could open up a US$100mn TAM for Genius
3M has been developing its EBO (expanded beam optical) technology since 2019 with
commercialization in late 2024 and deployment for Microsoft Azure data centers in July

2026. EBO is an upgrade to MPO connectors at the ends of fiber optics cables.  The
EBO is a lensed, non-contact ferrule that uses a prism lens to expand and collimate a
light beam to send across fiber before refocusing into a receiving fiber, improving dust
tolerance to cut cleaning steps and cable inspection times 85%. The technology was
supported by the MSA launched in May 2026, co-chaired by Oracle, with Meta, Senko
and Molex among the members for scale-out cabling. We saw opportunity opened for

Genius on the EBO ferrule, a small molded plastic part with a row of micro-lenses to
widen the light beam plus an anti reflection coating. This could open up a US$100mn
market TAM for Genius based on 4-12 ferrules per GPU (2-3 connections x 2-4 plugs per
connection) x 20mn accelerators x US$1 for the lens assembly.
Validation for its optical solution push with potential hyperscaler exposure
We believe the scale of placement at NT$4.8bn suggests scope to expand into CPO

where it could improve the MT ferrule used as the detachable plug end of the FAU to
hold the fiber in position when it is connected to a switch adapter or removable cable.
Genius  collaboration with 3M can bring the company closer to the end demand with its
direct partnership with Microsoft and bring revenue contribution forward to 2027,
ahead of Genius CPO ramp in 2028E. For CPO, we still see potential to reach 10% of the
TAM supplying MT ferrule, prism and V-groove.

Reiterate Buy with PT at NT$1,250
Our recent update on Genius had raised the PT from NT$700 to NT$1,250 reflecting the
company's potential CPO market with its progress sampling three precision components
inside the fiber array unit (FAU) targeting FAU integrators such as Coherent and FOCI.
The collaboration strengthens our confidence on Genius capability within the optical
connectivity industry, and reiterate our Buy on the compony.

Company Description
Founded in 1989, Genius Electronic Optical (GSEO) is a leading global manufacturer of
camera lenses, providing full service optical solutions, including mobile lenses, automotive
lenses and AR/VR optics. Its production sites are in Taichung (one plant) and Xiamen (two

plants). The Taichung plant mainly manufactures VR lenses, while the Xiamen plants produce
compact lenses for consumer electronics. Optical lens products accounted for 73% of 2023
total revenue, and others include TOF lenses, flash lenses, VR lenses and LED lighting
products. Its major client is Apple and it supplies lenses for iPhones and iPads.
UBS EPS and Consensus EPS
The UBS EPS is an adjusted diluted EPS metric. It is calculated using the UBS analyst's

interpretation of earnings suitable for valuation purposes divided by the diluted number of
shares. This may differ to the way the consensus EPS metric has been calculated.
Valuation Method and Risk Statement
Our price target is based on PE-based sum-of-the-parts model.
Genius' growth is highly leveraged to the overall handset industry and could be affected by
volatility in handset demand, as well as whether handset OEMs will continue upgrading their

camera design. Genius' margins could be affected by increasing competition and the rise of
new technologies, ASP pressure from customers and fluctuating material and labour costs.
Downside risks include competitors catching up in technology, yield rate issues for new
products and uncertain iPhone demand.

the relevant offering materials for that investment. Structured investments are unsecured obligations of a particular issuer with returns linked to the performance of an
underlying asset. Depending on the terms of the investment, investors could lose all or a substantial portion of their investment based on the performance of the underlying
asset. Investors could also lose their entire investment if the issuer becomes insolvent. UBS does not guarantee in any way the obligations or the financial condition of any

issuer or the accuracy of any financial information provided by any issuer. Structured investments are not traditional investments and investing in a structured investment is
not equivalent to investing directly in the underlying asset. Structured investments may have limited or no liquidity, and investors should be prepared to hold their investment
to maturity. The return of structured investments may be limited by a maximum gain, participation rate or other feature. Structured investments may include call features and,

if a structured investment is called early, investors would not earn any further return and may not be able to reinvest in similar investments with similar terms. Structured
investments include costs and fees which are generally embedded in the price of the investment. The tax treatment of a structured investment may be complex and may differ
from a direct investment in the underlying asset. UBS and its employees do not provide tax advice. Investors should consult their own tax advisor about their own tax situation

before investing in any securities.
Important Information About Sustainable Investing Strategies: Sustainable investing strategies aim to consider and incorporate environmental, social and governance
(ESG) factors into investment process and portfolio construction. Strategies across geographies approach ESG analysis and incorporate the findings in a variety of ways.
Incorporating ESG factors or sustainable investing considerations may inhibit the portfolio manager’s ability to participate in certain investment opportunities that otherwise

would be consistent with its investment objective and other principal investment strategies. The returns on a portfolio incorporating ESG factors or sustainable investing
considerations may be lower or higher than portfolios where ESG factors, exclusions, or other sustainability issues are not considered by the portfolio manager, and the
investment opportunities available to such portfolios may differ.

Greater China Technology Semiconductors
Asia Pacific
Industry View Attractive
ICA/IPO: Gaining traction, but cost still a hurdle. A China CSP outlined optical
form factors from XPO/NPO to interposer optics (IPO) and CPO, including

integration of IPO with interposer copper attach (ICA). TE Connectivity showcased
224G/448G NPO/CPO sockets and ICA sockets with 0.4mm pitch and up to 10,000
pins, offering high-density connectivity, low crosstalk and improved reworkability.
We see rising traction for ICA/IPO, with potential mass production in 2027   (For
more, see our recent CPO report).
Marvell IMC: Complementary to AST2700, not a replacement. Marvell
demonstrated the OCTEON-based infrastructure management controller (IMC)

addresses higher-level Al rack/cluster telemetry and control; ASPEED's AST2700
BMC continues to handle node-level security and remote management. Given
distinct functions and software ecosystems, we do not view IMC as a direct
AST2700 competitor. We also don't see an AST2700 production issue, supporting
an on-track ramp. The key debate is actually market share in TPU/Axion.
Caliptra: Expanding hardware Root of Trust opportunities. The open Caliptra

framework defines silicon-level Root of trust (RoT) for data-center processors and
accelerators, supporting secure boot, device identity and attestation eMemory
(3529.TW) subsidiary PUFsecurity offers PUFrt, providing NeoPUF-derived device,
NeoFuse OTP secure storage and hardware entropy as physical security building
blocks for Caliptra implementations. Broader adoption could support incremental
PUF/security-IP licensing, although monetization depends on qualifications and

customer design wins. (For more, see our quantum security report).
Other: CXL fabric, cooling and compute ramps: The microchannel lid
demonstration underscored the importance of chip-level thermal design as rack

Key Takeaways
ICA/IPO traction: China CSP and TE demonstrations suggest growing adoption,
although cost could be higher
IMC versus BMC: Marvell IMC complements AST2700; we see no production
disruption.
Hardware security: Caliptra strengthens the case for eMemory PUF-based
security IP.
CXL and thermal: CXL supports memory pooling: Cooling and ASIC/CPU ramps

remain in focus. Muse is likely driving storage server demand.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O or Over) - The stock's total return is expected to exceed the total return of the relevant country MSCI Index or the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry
team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis over the next 12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E or Equal) - The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the total return of the relevant country MSCI Index or the average total return of the analyst's industry (or

industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis over the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR) - Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the relevant country MSCI Index or the average total return of the analyst's
industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U or Under) - The stock's total return is expected to be below the total return of the relevant country MSCI Index or the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry

team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.

## Document 122: 261026_mq_tpca

Source PDF: all report pdf/261026_mq_tpca.pdf
Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-10-26

### Page 1

Flashnote
26 October 2026
PCB / Substrates
TPCA takeaways - PTFE and expanding supply
tightness
What's new
• We attended the TPCA show during Oct 20-22 and hosted meetings with
industry experts. Product readiness/adoption between PTFE and M9-M11
CCL (Copper Clad Laminate) was the most discussed topic. Investors also
tried to find new stock ideas under expanding industry shortages from
production equipment, raw materials, to PCB products.
Why it matters

• PTFE - PCB yield the main bottleneck while alternative solutions catch
up: Nvidia showcasing PTFE (Polytetrafluoroethylene) NVLink board at
OCP (Oct 14) revived the discussion about PTFE CCL used in Nvidia switch
trays. While PTFE posses exceptionally low Dk (latency) and Df (signal loss)
for a CCL material, PCB processability and production yield remain the
biggest hurdles due to its softness and high CTE (Coefficient of Thermal
Expansion) that leads to misregistration, warpage and difficulty of drilling/

lamination. Our checks with some Nvidia PCB partners suggest <10% yield
rate currently. ESG risk is another issue for wide adoption. Meanwhile,
traditional CCL is migrating to M9/10/11, with or without Qglass/glass fibre,
improving electrical performance with relatively better PCB processability.
We believe PTFE is more of a transitional/niche alternative before the
traditional CCL solution further improves.
• Copper foil and substrate's CCL could be the next bottleneck: We

continue to see tightness or shortages across the PCB supply chain, and
end customers are making prepayment/capex sponsorships (for substrates)
or signing LTAs to secure the supply. We expect continued price hikes for
ABF substrates in 2027-28, and likely the same for PCB's CCL, due to supply
tightness and fast migrations. During our exchanges with PCB experts, high-
end copper foil (HVLP4 or above) is getting tighter as adoption increases on
new AI server launches, which could lead to price hike towards end of the

year. And after Tglass shortage eases on Nittobo (3110 JP, NR)'s expansion
in 2027, substrate's CCL could become the next bottleneck. This aligns with
EMC's strategy to expand its substrate's CCL business from 2027.
What now
• We believe EMC's recent correction on PTFE is overdone as the technology
is still far from mature and company also has PTFE-hybrid solutions under
development alongside M9/10 upgrades. Its substrate's CCL business also
looks well-positioned under increasing industry tightness and needs for

diversification. For the CCL supply chain, we also like Doosan as the main
Nvidia compute tray CCL supplier.
• For substrates, we prefer leading players like Unimicron and Ibiden on
higher AI exposure to benefit more from the spec upgrades and industry
shortages.
Technology Hardware & Equipment

### Page 3

Doosan (000150 KS) • Delayed CCL capacity ramp-up.
• Higher-than-expected cash outlays for the remaining 29% stake in Siltron.
• Lower valuations of affiliate holdings.
Elite Material (2383 TT) • Potential supply bottlenecks for AI server value chain or a slowdown in AI demand.
• Lower efficiency or higher costs during the ramp-up of new capacity across multiple locations.
• More frequent raw material price increases that the company may not be able to pass through to average selling prices in a timely

manner.
Ibiden (4062 JP) • Delays in capex for AI servers and data centres.
• Slower price increases relative to global peers.
• Technological catch-up by peers in high-end FC-BGA.
• Delays in adoption of new technologies (glass-core substrates and CPO substrates).
• Lower automotive production volumes and weaker demand in the ceramics business.
• A stronger yen, which would be negative.
Kinsus Interconnect (3189 TT) • Slower margin expansion on steeper material price hikes.

• Delayed shipment for new projects (e.g., Rubin) or new ASIC customers.
• T-glass shortages hinder shipment growth.
• Potential share dilution from the private placement (up to 20% dilution) is not factored in our forecasts.
Unimicron Technology (3037 TT) • Tglass supply constraints on ABF substrate growth.
• Slower-than-expected capacity expansion due to execution challenges or longer equipment lead times.
• Increased competition from tier-two substrate makers in the AI server market.

## Document 134: 261027_daiwa_TSMT

Source PDF: all report pdf/261027_daiwa_TSMT.pdf
Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-10-27
Pages: 4
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

Taiwan Information Technology

Background: We met with Taiwan Surface Mounting Technology (TSMT) 
on 27 October. According to management, its product mix for 3Q26 was 
TFT-LCD 47%, Mini LED 9%, DRAM module 15%, auto 10%, cable 7%, 
touch 4% and others 8%. TSMT is positive on its optical transceiver, DRAM 
module, auto, low-earth orbit satellites (LEO), server and robotic segments. 
The QFII holding in TSMT was 29.7% on 22 October vs. 19.8% at the end 
of 2025.

Highlights: 4Q26 and 2027 outlook update – an upward revision. 
TSMT guided for its 4Q26 revenue to grow by 5-8% QoQ, and 2027 
revenue to grow by at least 20% (some upside potential subject to raw 
material conditions) for 2027 thanks to multiple revenue drivers, including 
optical transceivers, DRAM module, auto, LEO, server and robotic 
segments. For 4Q26 and 2027, the company expects its quarterly revenue 
and gross margin to further expand sequentially on a more favourable 
product mix from optical transceiver, LEO and server revenue. TSMT 
expects its gross margin to improve to 15-17% in 1H27 and over 20% in 
2H27 on the solid optical transceiver order outlook.

Strong revenue and margin outlook is expected to be driven mainly 
by the optical transceiver segment. TSMT mentioned several revenue 
drivers for 2027-28; among them, optical transceiver surface mounting 
technology (SMT) assembly is the most significant one. TSMT is a major 
global supplier for 800G and 1.6T products. A US-based client has noted 
that the 1.6T delivery schedule was ahead of its previous expectation (from 
4Q26 to 3Q26). The company has thus won an additional US-based tier-
one client (expected to have a larger contribution than the first optical client 
from 3Q27) and a China-based client over the past few months. TSMT is 
also in talks with a new US-based tier-one optical transceiver client. 
According to TSMT, optical transceiver revenue will likely account for 5-
10% of total revenue in 1Q27, 10-15% in 2Q27 and 15-20% in 3Q27; for 
2028, the company aims for 30-40%. Given the pure outsourcing (ie, 
includes no buying and selling of material) nature of the optical transceiver 
business, the segment brings upside potential to the gross margin level, 
per management. Aside from the optical transceiver segment, the company 
also sees strong revenue momentum in 2027-2028 from the DRAM module 
(2027 revenue up 30+% YoY), auto, robotic, LEO and server segments. 
TSMT says it has been expanding its global capacity and adjusting its 
equipment to fit different usages, eg, transforming from TFT-LCD to optical 
transceiver.

Valuation: The stock is currently trading at PERs of 24.8x/13.8x, based on 
Bloomberg’s 2026E/27E EPS forecasts.

27 October 2026

6274 台燿 
(Taiwan Union Technology Co.)

報告日期： 2026/10/08 研 究 員：方姿婷  (Lesley Fang) 
壹、投 資 建 議 
重點摘要： 
 2Q26 營收符合預期， 毛利率受 1H26 漲價提升 ， 預期 3Q26 毛利
將順利反應成本上漲與優化之產品組合 ， 毛利率預估提升 3%+ 。  
 AI 伺服器帶動高階銅箔基板需求強勁，同時驅動高階網路交換
器升級動能，銅箔基板需要更高速傳輸、訊號低損耗之下會陸
續採用 CCL M8、M9 規格。 
 2H26 低軌 S 客戶 V3 版本新型火箭搭載低軌衛星，所採用之銅
箔基板升級至 M8 等級；以及，1.6T 光模組需求量增帶動銅箔
基板需求量增。

一. 2Q26 營收與財報符合市場預期，3Q26 營收優於預期  
台燿 2Q26 營收為 143.01 億元，+42.2%QoQ、+110.93%YoY，毛利
率為 29.72% ，稅後淨利為 23.42 億元，單季稅後 EPS+7.82 元。2Q26
台燿受到 ASIC CCL 專案出貨帶動，產品組合優化，毛利率已相較
1Q26 季增 4.5% 。台燿已公告 3Q26 營收為 179.48 億元(優於市場預
期 3%)，+25.50%QoQ，毛利率可順利轉嫁且高階 CCL 出貨比重提
升，預估台燿 3Q26 毛利率為 33.21%，稅後淨利 36.72 億元，單季
稅後 EPS+12.60 元。

二. AI 伺服器帶動 CCL/PCB 產值提升，產能擴充 33% 
宏遠投顧觀察，AI 伺服器與先進封裝等需求日增，高階 CCL 市場
需求將在 2024 年~2026 年 CAGR 達 26% ， 而高階 CCL 供給端 CAGR
則是 7%，高階市場需求殷切。高階銅箔基板(包含 AI 伺服器和高
階網路交換器，自 2024 年始採用 M8 CCL 材料；2026 年 M8 材料
佔總體 CCL 市場 40%，M8 材料佔比顯著高於 2022 年、2023 年的
22%和 26%。台系銅箔基板供應鏈當中，M6~M8 等高階材料的供
應廠商為(2383)台光電為首、(6274)台燿次之。回顧 3 年來，台燿
產能擴充進程，端視泰國廠進度而定。4Q26 泰國新廠產能陸續開
出，台燿總產能為 260 萬張/月，+13%YoY。展望 2027 年，台燿泰
國廠、常熟廠初步先提升 30 萬張/月，逐步提升總新增產能達 120
萬張/月，預估 2027 年底時，總產能達 380 萬張/月，+46%YoY；預
估 2028 年底時，台燿總產能擴充至 530 萬張/月，+39.5%YoY，平
均 3 年每年產能擴充約 33% 。高階 CCL 在 AI 伺服器、高速網路交

三.  台燿高階網路 Switch 板材、低軌衛星板材、1.6T 光
模塊板材，27 年規格朝向高階型號發展

CCL 規格，2H26 台燿將新增高階 Switch 客戶，隨著 AI 伺服器、
高階網路交換器產業發展朝向 800G、1.6T Switch 升級趨勢帶動之
下， 
銅箔基板需要更高速傳輸、訊號低損耗之下會陸續採用 CCL M8、
M9 規格。此外，2H26 開始 800G 與 1.6T 光纖收發模組(採用 M8、
M9 CCL)開始放量，AI 資料中心帶動光纖收發模組需求，400G 以
上光纖收發模組出貨量 2026 年 1 億個、2027 年 1.45 億個、2028 年
達到 1.6~1.65 億個；在中系系統廠商積極拉貨之下，1.6T 光纖收
發模組 2026/2027/2028 年估量 15~20M/55~60M/85~100M ， 1.6T 光纖
收發模組，於 2 年內呈倍數增長。低軌大型 S 客戶 V3 版本新型火
箭搭載低軌衛星 ， 所採用之銅箔基板升級至 M8 等級 ， 材料損耗要
求降低，訊號傳出完整性標準提高，對於 CCL、PCB(HDI)、玻纖
與銅箔等規格預期同步升級，推升材料成本與 PCB 產值。

四.預估 2026/2027 年稅後 EPS 38.49/66.22 元 
2026 年受惠 AI 伺服器及 ASIC 需求持續強勁的帶動下，台燿為網
通、高階網路交換器銅箔基板重要供貨廠商，將受惠 AI Server 對

EPS+38.49元 。 隨泰國廠產能釋出 ， 預估台燿 2027年營收增至 963.61
億元，+55.73%YoY，毛利率為 33.26%，稅後淨利 198.23 億元，稅
後 EPS+66.22 元。

與相關供應的零組件商機，以及攻入 AI 伺服器、AI 高階網路交換

貳、公 司 概 況

一. 公司簡介 
台燿(6274)為銅箔基板廠商，主要產製雙面印刷電路板與多層印刷
電路板之銅箔基板 、 內層銅箔基板 、 黏合片 ， 產品應用別當中 ， ELL
與 SLL(M7、M8)佔比 39%、VLL 與 LL(M4、M6)佔 31%、HDI 與 N-
LL 佔 16%、其他品項佔 14%。

二.2026~2027 年營運概況及展望 
1.HDI 與 M8 以上升級仍為關注焦點，2026~2028 年產能建置積極 
過往一般型伺服器用板採用 HDI 設計，強調細線路設計，CPU 尺
寸變大 Pin Count 數量增加至 2,000 只~4,000 只 Pin Count，PCB 板
面積不變之下要走向細線路化(線寬/線距)朝向更細線路設計。製
程上 HDI 經過多次壓合後，PP 在熱壓多次後，每一層熱膨脹係數
不同，導致通孔 Crack 或翹曲，製作時容易產生爆板，因此對於
PCB 板廠製作良率會是一大技術難關，而金像電、健鼎等廠商於
高階 HDI 相對具備生產優勢與競爭力，相對應台光電、台燿等高
階 CCL 供貨與升級也成為製程一大瓶頸；回顧 3 年來，台燿產能
擴充進程，端視泰國廠進度而定。4Q26 泰國新廠產能陸續開出，
台 燿 總 產 能 為 260 萬 張 /月，+13%YoY。展 望 2027 年，台 燿 泰 國 廠、
常熟廠初步先提升 30 萬張/月，逐步提升總新增產能達 120 萬張/
月 ， 預估 2027 年底時 ， 總產能達 380 萬張/月 ， +46%YoY ； 預估 2028
年底時，台燿總產能擴充至 530 萬張/月，+39.5%YoY，平均 3 年
每年產能擴充約 33%。高階 CCL 在 AI 伺服器、高速網路交換器
(高階 Switch)帶動之下，一旦，泰國廠產能擴充可望使得台燿市佔
率得以提升。2027 年隨著 Nvidia Rubin 平台將帶動 CCL M8+、M9
材料出貨攀升，AI ASIC CCL 拉貨動能呈現 50~60%高度成長，台
光電與台燿等高階 CCL 新產能釋出，產品組合持續優化。

針對 AI GPU 與 ASIC 發展進程，Google TPU 出貨量從 2026 年 400
萬顆上修至 2027 年(V8 增量)增至 800 萬顆，倍數增長。AWS ASIC 
預計 2027 年出貨量達 330~350 萬顆，增長+21%YoY 。NVIDIA 體系
GPU，Rubin 系列層數提升至 24~26L，材料端從 M8/M8+往更高規
格升級，更高層數與多層式架構，促使 PCB 板訊號走線、供電完
整性、高速訊號之阻抗匹配控制、訊號傳輸完整性，皆帶動 PCB

## Document 216: [005930] 삼성전자(005930) 현저한 저평가삼성전자(005930) 현저한 저평가현저한 저평가 - 기업

Source PDF: all report pdf/[005930] 삼성전자(005930) 현저한 저평가삼성전자(005930) 현저한 저평가현저한 저평가 - 기업.pdf
Report source: Hankyung
Date: 2026-10-06
Pages: 9
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

00

삼성전자 (005930/KS) 
현저한 저평가 
3Q26 영업이익 106 조원 예상 
삼성전자의 3Q26 영업이익을 106조원 (+18% QoQ)으로 예상한다. 이는 기존 당사 
전망치 111 조원 대비 4.5% 하향된 것이다. 원/달러 환율 가정을 기존 1,450원에서 
1,400원으로 3.4% 하향, MX/NW 사업부의 적자 폭을 확대 반영 (-0.5조원 -> -
1.4조원)했기 때문이다. 주가의 핵심인 메모리 판가는 DRAM +17%, NAND +15%
로 오히려 예상을 소폭 상회할 것으로 예상된다. 출하는 수요 강세에도 불구하고 재고 
부족, HBM 확대에 따른 제한적 공급 여력으로 DRAM +4%, NAND +7%의 기존 예
상을 유지한다. HBM은 분기 최대 출하를 경신하기 시작할 것이다. 또한 AI 강세로 파
운드리 수주 및 가동률의 동반 상승이 지속 중이다. 성과 충당금 반영 등에 따라 단기 
흑자전환 가시성은 제한적이나, DS 사업부 전체적으로 강화되고 있는 국면이다.

2027 년 568 조원 (+55% YoY) 전망 유지. 환율 하락 반영 
SK증권은 삼성전자의 2027년 영업이익 전망치를 568조원으로 유지한다. 원/달러 환
율 가정을 기존 1,440원에서 1,340원으로 7% 하향했음에도, 실적 전망을 유지하는 
이유는 AI 메모리 수요 강세 속 공급 부족 심화, LTA에 따른 Dual market 효과에 의
한 Commodity 메모리 가격 강세, 2027년 HBM 판가 강세 (+103% YoY) 와 출하 
강세, 수익성 회복의 동반, 파운드리 적자 폭 축소의 본격화 전망 때문이다. 2027년 메
모리 출하는 DRAM +22%, NAND +16%, 판가는 DRAM +38%, NAND +28%
를 예상한다. 시장에서 우려하는 메모리 가격 하락, 감익 시나리오는 없을 것이다.

차별적 실적과 주주환원 본격화. 현저한 저평가 
SK증권은 메모리 업황과 삼성전자의 주가가 현저히 저평가 되어있다고 판단한다. 삼성
전자의 2027E P/E는 4.2X에 불과하며, 이익 창출력은 글로벌 최상위권이다. 주주환
원 정책 또한 기존의 FCF 50% 환원이 향후에도 유지될 것으로 가정한다면, 2027년 
193조원, 2028년 222조원으로 현재 시가총액 (우선주 포함) 대비 각각 11%, 13% 
수준에 육박한다. 성장을 위한 투자를 확대하고 있음에도, 대규모의 환원이 가능하다. 
LTA는 AI 시대 메모리 수요의 질과 실적 가시성을 제고시키며, 이는 주주환원의 지속
가능성을 의미한다. 메모리 가격의 높은 기저, LTA 물량의 계약 가격 적용을 감안하면, 
가격 상승률 자체는 안정화될 것이다. 다만 실적 안정성, 가시성, Duration을 확보하기 
시작했다는 점이 훨씬 중요한 가치이며, 아직 시장의 Playbook에 없을 뿐이다. 시대는 
변하고 있고, 시장도 변해야 한다. 투자의견 BUY, 목표주가 610,000원을 유지한다. 
2026-10-06

반도체. 한동희 / donghee.han@sks.co.kr / 3773-8826

본 보고서에 언급된 종목의 경우 당사 조사분석담당자는 본인의 담당종목을 보유하고 있지 않습니다. 
본 보고서는 기관투자가 또는 제 3 자에게 사전 제공된 사실이 없습니다. 
당사는 자료공표일 현재 해당기업과 관련하여 특별한 이해 관계가 없습니다. 
종목별 투자의견은 다음과 같습니다.

## Document 241: AI募资狂潮“最大赢家”：高盛依旧是“华尔街之王”

Source PDF: all report pdf/AI募资狂潮“最大赢家”：高盛依旧是“华尔街之王”.pdf
Report source: WallstreetCN (Global)
Date: 2026-10-27
Pages: 2
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

单⽇三笔、合计逾 2100 亿美元，⾼盛的名字出现在每⼀笔 AI 巨型融资交易之中：联合主导
Meta 950 亿美元股票发⾏、独揽 SpaceX 400 亿美元芯⽚融资结构安排、出任 Anthropic
IPO 左⼀账簿管理⼈。 AI 融资超级周期正在重塑华尔街竞争格局，⾼盛已率先将关系优势变
现为真⾦⽩银。
摘要：
在 AI 融资热潮席卷华尔街之际，⾼盛正以压倒性姿态确⽴其市场主导地位。
周⼀，三笔总规模⾄少达 2100 亿美元的 AI 巨型融资交易同时落地，⾼盛的名字出现在每⼀笔交
易之中。这⼀天，⾼盛联合主导了 Meta Platforms ⾼达 950 亿美元的股票发⾏，担任 SpaceX
400 亿美元芯⽚融资交易的唯⼀结构安排⾏，并出任 Anthropic ⾸次公开募股（ IPO ）的左⼀账簿
管理⼈ —— 后者的规模预计⾄少与 SpaceX 今年 6 ⽉的上市相当。
这三笔交易将为⾼盛带来⾄少数千万美元、甚⾄可能⾼达数亿美元的收⼊。
AI 融资热潮正在重塑华尔街的竞争格局。华尔街五⼤⾏今年前九个⽉的承销收⼊合计已超过 190

亿美元，其中股权资本市场收⼊同⽐激增 48% ，债务承销收⼊增⻓ 13% 。⾼盛凭借在 AI ⽣态系
统中的深度布局，正在将这场超级周期转化为实实在在的业务优势。
单⽇ 2100 亿美元，三笔交易尽显主导地位
周⼀的三笔交易，集中展⽰了⾼盛在 AI 融资领域的全⾯渗透。
在 Meta 交易中，⾼盛担任联合主导⾏，参与这笔华尔街近年来规模最⼤的股票发⾏之⼀。在
SpaceX 交易中，⾼盛不仅是唯⼀结构安排⾏，其资产管理部⻔旗下基⾦也直接出资参与 —— 这
笔交易由 SpaceX 向英伟达采购芯⽚，并由阿波罗全球管理公司提供租赁⽀持。据⼀位知情⼈⼠
透露，该交易的起点是数⽉前 SpaceX 主动致电⾼盛。
Anthropic IPO 的安排则令部分市场⼈⼠感到意外。此前，摩根⼠丹利⼀直主导 Anthropic 债务循
环信贷额度的扩容⼯作，外界普遍预期其将在 IPO 中占据核⼼位置。据知情⼈⼠透露，⾼盛与
Anthropic 的关系由旧⾦⼭的银⾏家 Nick Giovanni 和 Jung Min 主导，后者同时也是英伟达的核⼼

银⾏家。
深耕关系多年， AI 超级周期厚积薄发
⾼盛在 AI 融资领域的强势地位，并⾮⼀蹴⽽就，⽽是多年关系积累的结果。
今年 6 ⽉，⾼盛担任 SpaceX IPO 的左⼀账簿管理⼈，凭借 Dan Dees 、 Kim Posnett 、 David
Ludwig 和 Susie Scher 等资深银⾏家多年的关系经营，获得 1 亿美元承销费。据彭博本⽉早些时
候报道，⾼盛还从其为富裕客⼾安排的早期投资中获得了数亿美元的激励费收⼊。
在 Alphabet ⽅⾯，⾼盛担任⾕歌⺟公司逾 800 亿美元股票发⾏的顾问，这是史上规模最⼤的股
票发⾏之⼀，核⼼银⾏家 Posnett 和 Yasmine Coupal 同时也主导了周⼀的 Meta 交易。
为系统性整合旗下各业务条线的资源优势，⾼盛去年专⻔成⽴了资本解决⽅案团队，由 Pete
Lyon 和 Mahesh Saireddy 领衔，统筹协调资产管理、投资银⾏乃⾄交易业务，为客⼾构建⼤型
综合融资⽅案。

## Document 260: Bank bond supply to stay at cruising altitude in 2027

Source PDF: all report pdf/Bank bond supply to stay at cruising altitude in 2027.pdf
Report source: ING Think
Date: 2026-10-21
Pages: 8
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

We expect EUR bank bond supply to reach €450bn in 2027.
Redemptions remain the main driver of the high supply, but
the modest macroeconomic outlook is not expected to push
supply up any further. We foresee covered bond issuance
reaching €185bn, senior unsecured bond supply at €210bn and
another €55bn in subordinated bank bonds to be printed

Marine Leleux and Jesse Norcross
High redemption volumes are expected to keep EUR bank bond supply
elevated in 2027
Variables driving next year's bank bond
supply
We expect EUR bank bond supply to reach €450bn in 2027, down
slightly from the €455bn forecast for 2026. In our view, several
variables will be steering next year’s issuance.
Redemptions will remain the central driver of the elevated 2027
bank bond supply forecast. Nearly €400bn of bonds mature in
2027, up €30bn year-on-year and the highest level in a decade.

### Page 2

The increase is especially signiﬁcant in the covered bond segment
where €186bn will mature, a historic high.
The subordinated segment will also rise, with redemptions totalling
€45bn, up about €6bn from 2026. Of this total, €31bn is in Tier 2
instruments and €14bn in AT1s. The senior unsecured segment will
partially oﬀset these increases, with total redemptions reaching
more than €162bn, down from more than €170bn recorded this
year. The majority stems from senior bail-in bonds (senior non-

preferred or senior HoldCo) with more than €90bn and an
additional €71bn maturing in senior preferred instruments.
Despite diverging trends across segments, elevated redemption
volumes remain the main driver of bank issuance in the euro bond
primary market, as institutions reﬁnance maturing instruments.

seen after the pandemic.

Central Bank rate hike in 2026, bringing policy rates to 2.75% in
early 2027. The higher interest rate environment is not expected to

Asset swap spread levels initially proved very resilient to

geopolitical instability and related economic changes this year.
Despite some volatility in March, following the outbreak of the US-
Israel conﬂict with Iran, spread levels recovered quickly and reached
levels close to their historic low. However, a broader escalation of
the conﬂict across the Middle East, coupled with the absence of a
clear path to resolution, could put further pressure on spreads.
In addition, budget and political concerns in France and OAT-Bund

volatility, as well as broader European ﬁscal concerns, have recently
led to spreads widening again. The combination of French political
uncertainty and the Middle East conﬂict could trigger some periods
of volatility in the market in 2027. However, we believe these
uncertainties will not be a strong negative for bank bond supply.
Depending on the timing, though, periodic widening may make
ﬁnancial institutions less keen to pre-fund redemptions coming up

in 2028.

Our estimate includes €185bn in covered bonds, comprising
€180bn in benchmark-sized issuances, including ﬂoating rate
notes, and €5bn in sub-benchmark bonds. This is a €5bn drop
versus our 2026 forecast. It reﬂects the pre-ﬁnancing already made
by issuers this year in preparation for the historically high
redemptions.
Furthermore, next year’s mortgage lending forecasts point to
stagnation, somewhat limiting any further increase in covered

bond issuance. Besides, with the securitisation package entering its
ﬁnalisation stage, issuers with high funding needs may shift some
secured issuance towards securitisation for diversiﬁcation
purposes. Increasing demand for covered bonds from overseas
investors driven by regulatory developments may also result in
more currency diversiﬁcation, away from EUR-denominated
issuance.
We forecast senior unsecured supply to reach €210bn next year.
This is split between €70bn in senior preferred bonds and €140bn in

senior bail-in instruments. It represents a €10bn YoY decline in

### Page 5

senior preferred supply, reﬂecting both the drop in the segment’s
redemptions from 2027 onwards and the potential impact of the
Crisis Management and Deposit Insurance (CMDI) framework. As the
CMDI reforms are expected to enter into force in 2028, they could
reduce the attractiveness of senior preferred bonds versus senior
bail-in instruments in some EU countries, as senior preferred
exposures will rank lower than, and no longer pari passu to,
uncovered deposits in the creditor hierarchy. The funding cost

advantage of senior preferred unsecured issuance could decline. At
the same time, issuing senior non-preferred bonds could serve the
purpose of increasing the bail-inable layer protecting preferred
senior bonds and their current ratings. This could shift some
issuers’ focus towards the senior non-preferred bucket instead of
the senior preferred segment.
Our forecast for senior non-preferred supply remains stable
compared to our 2026 estimate. While redemptions will increase by

€6bn in 2027 and further in 2028 to reach €102bn, we believe the
impact of higher reﬁnancing needs on non-preferred senior supply
will be partially oﬀset by the lack of balance sheet growth against
the backdrop of higher interest rates. Furthermore, the
outperformance of US dollar debt versus EUR-denominated debt
could see issuers turn more often towards the USD market,
potentially negatively aﬀecting EUR senior bail-in supply.
Lastly, we expect Tier 2 supply to reach €35bn and AT1 supply to

reach €20bn in 2027. This is a €5bn increase YoY in both AT1 and
Tier 2 compared to our 2026 forecast, mainly driven by higher
redemption levels. With AT1 redemptions in 2027 increasing by
€3bn YoY to €14bn, net supply is likely to be marginally higher next
year. In Tier 2, redemptions increase by €4bn to €31bn, essentially
meaning that net supply will only be marginally higher.
As the chart below highlights, net supply in AT1 has been
signiﬁcant in recent years but has slowed this year as the focus has

shifted towards reﬁnancing. As such, while we expect more AT1
supply next year compared to 2026, the levels are relatively in line
with the longer-term trend.

The main driver of supply will also be reﬁnancing for subordinated
capital. We expect a further marginal decline in CET1 buﬀers,
although most banks are already operating at target AT1 and Tier 2
ratios. Still, there are some issuers with substantial gaps in either
AT1 or Tier 2 ratios, which they may look to improve, leading to a

slight increase in supply.
Signiﬁcant Risk Transfer (SRT) issuance has also increased in recent
years and will continue to grow as a tool for banks in terms of
balance sheet and capital management, while it is also growing as
an investable asset class. Although SRTs provide capital and RWA
relief, they do not replace the gone-concern and loss-absorption
capacity of AT1 and Tier 2 instruments. As such, they play a more
complementary role in capital management, and we do not expect

it to have a major impact on bank capital issuance in 2027.
Bank M&A could also be a wildcard for 2027 issuance. With several
large M&A transactions potentially taking place, this may lead to
some capital optimisation and less incremental supply needs;
conversely, goodwill eﬀects, potential regulatory increases in buﬀer
requirements or RWA inﬂation may lead to a slight increase in the
need for capital. However, on the whole, we would expect M&A to

have a slight dampening eﬀect on supply.

## Document 327: GLOBAL MARKETS CALL_ Bond Yields Ease

Source PDF: all report pdf/GLOBAL MARKETS CALL_ Bond Yields Ease.pdf
Report source: researchmail轉寄
Date: 2026-10-25
Pages: 9
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

From:    Yardeni QuickTakes <yardeni-research@ghost.io>
Subject: GLOBAL MARKETS CALL: Bond Yields Ease
Date:    3 hours ago
GLOBAL MARKETS CALL: Bond Yields Ease

YARDENI QUICKTAKES
GLOBAL MARKETS CALL: Bond
Yields Ease
By Ed Yardeni & Toby Hearst • 25 Oct 2026
View in browser
Photo by Jeffrey Hamilton / Unsplash

Bond yields eased in most major markets last week, but France was the
glaring exception. In stocks, the global AI selloff kept hitting the equities
markets that had gained the most this year, putting the US back ahead of
the All Country World ex-US ytd. That supports our downgrade last week
of Go Global to market weight, though the fundamental case for overseas
stocks remains strong. Here's more:
I. Global Bonds

### Page 2

Yields fell in five of the eight major bond markets we track (chart). The US
10-year Treasury yield fell to 5.00%. France's yield jumped 24bps to
5.02%, slightly surpassing the US’s. Yields also fell in the UK, Australia,
Germany, and Japan.
The spread between French and German 10-year yields widened 31bps
last week to 167.9bps (chart). Last week, we wrote that contagion from
France had eased for now. It’s back. Italy’s spread widened to 123.9bps

from 106.6bps. Greece’s widened to 105.0bps from 90.8bps. Italy’s 10-
year bond now yields 44bps less than France’s.
II. Global Stocks
Brazil remains at the top of the country ETF rankings mtd, up 14.3% in
dollars (chart). South Korea remains at the bottom, down 17.2%. The US

### Page 3

is down just 0.3%, while the All Country World ex-US ETF is down 2.8%.
Go Global no longer leads Stay Home this year. The US ETF is up 11.4%
ytd, ahead of the All Country World ex-US ETF's 9.5% (chart). Taiwan
(65.1%) and South Korea (55.6%) still top the ytd rankings, followed by
Brazil (34.1%). China (-15.6%) and Indonesia (-43.3%) are at the bottom.

### Page 4

III. Global Earnings & Valuations
The US MSCI's share of the All Country World MSCI's market cap has
edged up to 65.0% (chart). The Emerging Markets (EM) MSCI’s share
slipped to 11.7% from 12.1% last week.
Yet the EM MSCI now accounts for 20.2% of the All Country World MSCI’s
forward earnings (chart). That’s more than Europe’s 15.0%. It’s the first

### Page 5

time that EM has overtaken Europe since the data began in 1995. The US
accounts for 54.4% of forward earnings, well below its market-cap share.
That mismatch shows up in valuations. The US MSCI’s forward P/E is
19.2 versus 12.2 for the All Country World ex-US (chart). The ex-US
multiple has dropped from 15.7 in January even as its forward earnings
soared. The EM MSCI trades at just 9.0.
IV. In Focus
(1) Brazil. Brazil’s MSCI stock price index is up 22.2% ytd in local

currency and 31.0% in dollars (chart). The real has gained 8.0% against
the dollar this year. In dollar terms, the index remains less than half its
2008 peak. Stocks rallied after Senator Flávio Bolsonaro took a surprise
lead in the October 4 first round. He held a narrow edge in the final polls

### Page 6

before the October 25 runoff against President Luiz Inácio Lula da Silva. A
Bloomberg poll released on October 19 gave Bolsonaro 51.1% of valid
votes against 48.9% for Lula.
Valuations aren’t stretched. Brazil’s forward P/E is 9.2 (chart). That’s
below its 2025 high of 10.7. The catch is that analysts expect earnings to
grow 44.6% this year but just 0.6% in 2027.
Brazil’s central bank has room to ease. It has lowered the Selic rate to
13.75% from a peak of 15.00% (chart). Inflation was 4.6% in September.

That leaves a real policy rate of about 9%.

### Page 7

(2) China. China’s property bust is deepening. The Shenzhen Real Estate
Stock Price Index fell to 953.55, breaking below the floor that had held
since 2012 (chart). Last week, we wrote that we would worry if it did that.
Now we are worried. Real estate investment is down 20.6% through the
first nine months of the year. Exports continue to carry the economy.
Beijing’s stabilization efforts haven’t stopped the selling. The China ETF
(FXI) fell 4.5% last week. Chinese technology stocks have missed the AI

rally that lifted US tech (chart). The China Technology ETF (CQQQ) is at
40.26, far below its 2021 peak.

### Page 8

China’s bond market confirms the deflationary picture. The 10-year
government bond yield is down to 1.62% (chart). It’s one of only two major
yields that have fallen this year. China’s yield is now well below Japan’s
2.80%.
💡

log in to the Yardeni QuickTakes website and post them at the end
of the QuickTakes article. Paid members' contributions may be
featured in our segment, "Ed Answers Your Questions".

Date: 2026-10-26

### Page 1

Markets reacted with relief after France avoided a rating
downgrade, but the story remains unchanged. With plenty of
political risks ahead, we remain wary of wider spreads

Michiel Tukker and Benjamin Schroeder
French bonds staged a rally after Moody's maintained France's ratings
France dodged a downgrade, but it doesn't
change the challenges ahead
French bond spreads extended Friday’s relief rally. The 10y
OAT/Bund spread narrowed by more than 10bp to below 155bp.
Not long ago, it was trading at its widest level since 2011. The
trigger was Moody’s refraining from downgrading France, which still
holds its double-A rating. It still comes with a negative outlook,
though. And eventually, France still needs to make a “signiﬁcant
and sustained ﬁscal eﬀort” to avoid a downgrade.
Fragmented politics and public unrest over the past weeks highlight
the challenge. We do not believe that we have seen the end of the
French market turmoil with uncertainty ahead of the elections next

April. A tail risk remains that public discontent is channelled into

For the ECB meeting this week, the latest retightening is still
welcome news as it removes some pressure for an interest rate hike.
Oﬃcials will almost surely be asked how they would treat the issue
if it were to escalate. This gives them more room for constructive
ambiguity, and would support our view that markets are unlikely to

After the French consumer conﬁdence data, we will be

conﬁdence, new home sales and the Richmond Fed business
indices.
The UK will auction £4.50bn of 5y gilts, while Italy will auction

will auction €5bn of 2y Schatz, while the US will auction

### Page 2

The Week Ahead October 16, 2026 
                                                                                                                                         2

CENTRAL BANK MEETINGS

People Bank of China (October 20)

We expect the PBOC to keep the 1Y LPR 
unchanged next week . Industrial 
production is expected to edge up from 
5.2% yoy in August to 5.3% in September, 
supported by firm external demand. 
Exports stayed strong, rising 36.3% yoy 
in September, driven by the regional AI -
electronics upcycle.

Domestic momentum, however, remains 
uneven. Retail sales growth is expected 
to moderate to 0.3% yoy  in September. 
Household sentiment is weak amid 
uncertain job prospects, slower income 
growth and elevated precautionary 
savings, while subdued property prices 
continue to weigh on household wealth 
and consumption. Investment also stays 
weak, with fixed as set investment 
expected to contract further by 7.5% yoy 
ytd in September. Corporate investment 
is cautious amid the anti -involution 
campaign and geopolitical tensions, 
while the property sector continues to be 
a major drag.

Policymakers are likely to maintain a 
measured policy stance, relying on 
targeted fiscal support to sustain growth 
rather than further rate cuts.

Bank Indonesia and Bangko Sentral ng 
Pilipinas (Oct 21 and 22).

Bank Indonesia is expected to extend its 
pause, as they lean on non-rate measures, 
including intervention and hedging 
facilities to help the currency.  On Friday, 
MSCI signaled a  potential shift in 
Indonesia’s status to a standalone 
status, rather than a reclassification to 
frontier market , after consultations are 
complete in mid -2027. Any ensuing risk -
off move in the currency , would likely 
prompt greater central bank vigilance 
and intervention.

Our base case for the benchmark rate to 
be held unchanged this quarter , backed 
by a lower conviction around back -to-
back US rate hikes in Oct and relative 
rupiah stability. Separately, the 
government announced additional 
support for low-income households in 4Q 
by way of in -kind assistance and wage 
subsidies. This year’s fiscal deficit i s 
likely to close in at -2.85% of GDP, close 
to our forecast at -2.9%, but few 
uncertainties facing the 2027 math (note).

We expect a 25bp hike from the BSP to 
5.25% in October , after three rate 
increases earlier in the year. Rising by the 
highest pace in Asia, Philippines inflation 
surged to 7.2% y/y in September from 6.1% 
in August, matching this year's peak and 
approaching a three -year high.  Inflation 
risks remain tilted to the upside, driven by 
further public transport fare adjustments, 
elevated oil prices, recent wage hikes 
and peso depreciation. Fiscal space is, 
meanwhile, limited to support 
households, via subsidies and/ instate 
price caps. Governor Remolona also 
signalled that the BSP remains ready to 
act on rates to contain inflation. With 
inflationary pressures likely to be 
persistent, the likelihood of further hikes 
in 4Q26 rises.

### Page 3

The Week Ahead October 16, 2026 
                                                                                                                                         3

Bank of Korea (October 22)

The Bank of Korea is expected to hold its 
policy rate at 3.00%, following back -to-
back rate hikes in July and August. AI -
driven growth momentum remains strong, 
as reflected in September export growth 
of around 80% YoY. However, the 
urgency for further tightening has eased, 
with CPI inflation stabilising at 2.9% YoY 
in September. The recent KOSPI 
correction and continued KRW strength 
also reduce the need for another near -
term hike. We maintain our call for a final 
25bp rate hike in November, bringing the 
policy rate to 3.25% by year-end.

FORTHCOMING DATA 
RELEASES

Japan

September trade and CPI data are due 
shortly. Export growth is expected to 
remain solid at 24% YoY, supported by 
continued strength in AI -related 
semiconductor exports. CPI inflation is 
projected to rise notably to 2.6% YoY, 
driven by the phase -out of publ ic utility 
subsidies, higher global energy prices, 
and persistent inflationary pressures 
from a weak yen. The BOJ is likely to 
increasingly acknowledge that its 2% 
inflation target is within reach. However, 
it is expected to keep rates unchanged 
at 1.25% at its October 29–30 meeting to 
assess the impact of the latest rate hike 
in September.

Taiwan

September export orders and industrial 
production data are due shortly. Export 
orders are expected to sustain 60% YoY 
growth, while industrial production is 
projected to expand by 20% YoY. The 
global AI boom faces growing headwinds 
from hyperscalers’ cash flow pressures, 
central bank rate hikes, and higher bond 
yields. However, our high -frequency 
indicators across AI demand (token 
expenditure), investment (hyperscaler  
capex and data centre construction 
spending), and supply (semiconductor 
sales and exports) show no clear signs of 
a significant slowdown so far.

Malaysia

Malaysia’s external demand likely 
remained strong in September 2026, 
while inflation edged up slightly. We 
expect export growth to sustain its 
robust expansion at 45% yoy , with 
momentum anchored by strong demand 
for electrical and electronics goods. Oil 
& gas shipments likely remained buoyant. 
However, high base effects prevented a 
faster pace of expansion.

We expect headline inflation to rise 
modestly to 2.1% yoy in September 2026. 
The renewed increase in global oil prices 
likely contributed to higher increases in 
domestic energy costs, although fuel 
subsidies continued to limit the cost 
pass-through and keep underlying 
inflationary pressures contained. Food 
inflation likely also firmed, with adverse 
weather conditions associated with El 
Nino posing upside risks.

### Page 4

The Week Ahead October 16, 2026 
                                                                                                                                         4

Singapore

We expect Singapore’s core and 
headline inflation to edge up slightly to 
2.3% yoy and 2.4% yoy , respectively, in 
September 2026, following modest 
increases to 2.2% yoy and 2.3% yoy in 
August. Inflationary pressures likely 
reflected firm private transport price 
increases amid higher petrol prices 
despite slower car price growth , the 
ongoing pass -through of higher global 
energy prices to fuel -exposed services 
items, positive contribution from 
elevated electricity & gas prices, firmer 
food inflation, and a slight increase in 
still-muted accommodation inflation. 
Firm, albeit not acc elerating, inflation in 
September 2026, which may be close to 
its peak in the Monetary Authority of 
Singapore’s assessment, likely validated 
its decision to maintain SGD NEER policy 
settings at the October 2026 review, 
following two proactive tightening 
moves in April and July.

Hong Kong

CPI is projected to edge up from 1.7% yoy 
in August to 1.9% in September, 
supported by improving consumption 
sentiment and higher global energy 
prices. Mainland visitor arrivals 
increased by 29.0% yoy in September on 
a daily average basis, providing furthe r 
support to retail spending and 
consumer-related services. Meanwhile, 
renewed conflict in the Middle East 
during the month has pushed up global 
energy prices, adding to upward 
pressure on utility and transport costs.

## Document 423: US Equity Views_ AI capex, required revenues, and what's priced in the AI trade

Source PDF: all report pdf/US Equity Views_ AI capex, required revenues, and what's priced in the AI trade.pdf
Report source: Goldman Sachs Publishing
Date: 2026-09-24
Pages: 16
Extraction status: ok

### Page 2

An update on AI and US equities

Both stocks and investor views relating to the AI trade have been exceptionally 
volatile during the last few months. Most of the volatility has been concentrated in the 
AI infrastructure complex, but the latest earnings season also drove sharp moves in the 
hyperscalers and among software stocks. 
Below we address three of the most common AI-related questions we have received 
from clients in recent weeks: 
1. What is the outlook for AI capex? 
2. What revenues are required to generate a sufficient return on recent AI 
investments? 
3. What outlook for AI is the equity market pricing?

1. What is the outlook for AI capex? 
The largest US hyperscalers are on track to spend $800 billion in capex in 2026 and 
consensus estimates imply they will spend $1.1 trillion in 2027. Consensus 2027 
capex estimates have been revised higher by $175 billion since the start of the Q2 
earnings season. Commentary from the hyperscalers during earnings season described a 
supply-constrained environment and large revenue backlogs, and these factors are 
driving the companies to tap both debt and equity markets to support additional 
investment spending. Based on consensus estimates, capex in 2027 is on track to reach a

### Page 3

larger share of GDP than any technological investment cycle since the railroad build-out 
in the late 1800’s. 
Our baseline forecast is that hyperscaler capex spending will increase substantially 
in 2027 but that the rate of both capex growth and upside surprises to consensus 
estimates will diminish relative to recent quarters. GS equity analysts estimate that 
hyperscaler capex will register $1.2 trillion in 2027 and $1.4 trillion in 2028. Their 
estimates are $130 billion and $100 billion above the consensus estimates, respectively, 
but nonetheless imply a deceleration in capex growth from nearly 100% in 2026 to 54% 
in 2027 and 12% in 2028.

The need for external financing and potential supply bottlenecks will likely 
constrain the rate of capex growth next year. Hyperscaler capex spending now 
exceeds cash flow from operations, which means large further growth will increasingly 
need funding via debt and equity issuance. While hyperscalers have added leverage, 
their balance sheets do not appear to be a binding constraint and their interest coverage 
ratios remain extremely healthy. Our credit strategists instead point to concentration in 
the investment grade market as the more relevant bottleneck, which will require 
additional funding from private credit and equity issuance. Potential government 
restrictions on data center construction or an intentional slowdown in frontier model 
roll-out could also act as potential constraints on spending, as could shortages of labor, 
power, memory, or other inputs. Constraints on capex represent a risk to the near-term 
earnings of AI infrastructure beneficiaries, but could help ease investor concerns about 
overbuilding.

### Page 4

Going forward, token demand growth will need to outpace declining token prices to 
support continued growth in investment spending. Frontier models are currently a 
key source of demand for hyperscaler compute. However, the rise of competitive 
open-source models has contributed to a decline in average token prices. Measures of 
frontier token demand slowed in July, simultaneous with sell-offs of many AI 
infrastructure stocks, but accelerated again in August. 
For most AI infrastructure companies, decelerating AI investment spending 
suggests decelerating EPS growth. AI infrastructure companies, and semis in 
particular, have been the clearest beneficiaries of the capex surge. Continued upward 
revisions to consensus 2027 capex estimates should support continued positive 
revisions to consensus 2027 EPS estimates for many AI infrastructure stocks, but the 
magnitude of revisions will likely diminish relative to what occurred in H1 2026.

### Page 5

2. What revenues are required to generate a return on AI investments? 
Our equity analysts estimate that the hyperscalers would need to generate annual 
AI revenues of roughly $300 billion in the next few years to break even on their 
average annual capex investments in 2026 and 2027. These estimates reflect their 
expectations for average annual AI capex in 2026 and 2027 for the US hyperscalers, 
including AMZN, GOOGL, META, MSFT, ORCL, and SPCX. The breakeven revenues 
required for this capex represent the sum of annual depreciation and operating 
expenses. Exhibit 8 provides an estimated sensitivity of required AI revenues the 
hyperscalers must generate in different return on invested capital (“ROIC”) scenarios. 
These required revenue estimates are anchored on average annual capex in 2026 and 
2027 and would argue for even larger estimates based on cumulative capex.

### Page 6

The hyperscalers’ current estimated AI revenues remain below the amount that 
would allow them to break even on their capex, but those revenues are growing 
quickly and revenue backlogs are sizable. The hyperscalers have started to monetize 
their AI investments. Year/year cloud revenue growth for AMZN, GOOGL, MSFT, and 
ORCL has accelerated from 25% in 2024 to 48% in Q2 2026. In dollar terms, annualized 
cloud revenues in excess of their 2024 growth rate registered nearly $70 billion in Q2 
and have been roughly doubling during the past few quarters. The three major US public 
cloud companies (AMZN, GOOGL, MSFT) have also announced a combined revenue 
backlog of $1.7 trillion, which provides line of sight into these companies’ ability to meet 
required revenue targets.

### Page 7

The magnitude of capex in 2026 and 2027 points to even larger “required” annual 
revenues in the next few years for the “application layer” of companies buying 
much of that compute from the hyperscalers. These revenues reflect the additional 
costs and profit margins of application companies on top of the “required” hyperscaler 
revenues selling compute. EBIT margins for large-cap software companies have 
averaged 40% during the past five years. We utilize EBIT margins rather than gross 
margins to include the costs of both training and inference, and assume additional 
expenses alongside compute. The range of potential required revenues is wide 
depending on which companies constitute the application layer and how their costs are 
structured.  
Exhibit 11 provides a range of potential “required” revenues based on hyperscaler 
ROIC and application layer EBIT margins. For example, the application layer would 
have to generate over $1 trillion in annual revenues if those companies had a 30% EBIT 
margin and the hyperscalers generated a 10%-20% return on invested capital. 
The “required” application layer revenues that would justify current capex are large 
but potentially achievable. For example, estimates of global advertising and software 
spending each equal roughly $1.5 trillion in 2026. Our economists estimate that total 
annual AI US labor productivity gains that will potentially accrue to capital total roughly 
$1.8 trillion. However, not all of these gains will necessarily accrue to the infrastructure 
and application layer companies. In addition, another year of strong capex growth in 
2028 would raise the hurdle further. AI-related revenues and capex needs would be 
substantially larger accounting for physical AI or the potential emergence of AGI.

### Page 8

3. What outlook for AI is the equity market pricing? 
While market participants express skepticism about the durability of AI 
infrastructure stocks’ earnings growth, that view is at least partially priced. The 
median AI infrastructure stock has de-rated from 32x consensus forward earnings in 
April 2026 to 22x today. In addition to tracking standard P/E multiples, comparing 
recent changes in market cap to recent changes in earnings can signal how long the 
market expects current earnings strength to persist. Among various AI infrastructure 
baskets, that ratio remains most elevated among optical networking, electrical grid, and 
liquid cooling stocks while expectations appear most conservative among 
semiconductors and utilities. 
Semiconductor valuations appear to embed particularly skeptical views regarding 
the sustainability of current earnings strength. Valuations have declined sharply 
among Semis stocks, which is typical among cyclical companies with strong near-term 
growth but uncertain medium-term growth. We believe the investment opportunity is 
more compelling in fabless chip stocks than in memory stocks. Our equity analysts are 
Buy-rated on NVDA and AVGO but Neutral-rated on MU.

### Page 9

Memory stock prices imply earnings will decline in coming years, but the degree of 
pessimism does not appear particularly extreme relative to past cycles. Memory 
stocks currently trade at a 2-year forward P/E of 4x, half their 15-year average, but 
Memory firms currently enjoy gross margins of roughly 80%, more than double their 
historical average. To bring the current P/E of memory stocks to its 15-year average, 
earnings in coming years would need to decline by roughly 50%. While this decline is 
large in absolute terms, memory is a cyclical industry and has experienced larger EPS 
declines, including outright negative earnings, during previous cycles. This pricing will 
prove too pessimistic if 2028 forecasts are realized, but many investors have expressed 
skepticism about rotating back into higher beta pockets of the infrastructure trade as 
capex growth begins to normalize.

### Page 10

In contrast, the fabless chip stocks also trade at discounted valuations but have had 
less extreme margin expansion. Margins for the largest fabless chip stocks are only 
modestly above their historical average. Earnings in the next few years would need to 
decline by about 10% to bring the P/E back to its historical average. This is similar to the 
magnitude of historical realized cyclical earnings declines for the group.

The likelihood of decelerating capex growth should be a positive catalyst for the 
hyperscalers. We previously laid out three conditions for the hyperscalers to regain 
leadership. These include decelerating macroeconomic growth, demonstrated 
monetization of AI investment, and decelerating capex growth. Our economists believe 
US economic growth is no longer accelerating. The hyperscalers have already started to 
demonstrate signs of monetization, and we expect capex growth will slow in 2027. 
Uncertainty about the length, magnitude, and return on investment for this capex cycle 
will likely persist. However, the hyperscalers have de-rated to their lowest P/E multiples 
in over a decade, and the group now trades just four turns above the median S&P 500 
company, its smallest premium on record. While investors have recently been focused 
more on the hyperscalers’ negative free cash flows than on their earnings, visibility into 
improving FCF as revenues accelerate and capex decelerates should be supportive for 
the stocks.

### Page 11

We expect the progression of the AI trade towards application roll-out will remain a 
source of return dispersion. We believe the “winners” and “losers” from the application 
layer will take several quarters to play out, as AI applications get built and adoption 
broadens. 
The threat of disruption had weighed on the share prices of many Software stocks 
earlier this year, but the group has rebounded sharply in recent weeks. Reported 
Software fundamentals have shown little sign of AI disruption, with annual recurring 
revenue growth for the median stock in GS analyst coverage accelerating slightly in 
recent quarters. A group of Software stocks has rallied by 45% and is now within 8% of 
its high reached in late 2025. Software stocks focused on IT infrastructure and 
cybersecurity have posted the strongest returns, while enterprise management software 
remain below their previous highs.

### Page 12

Consumer-facing businesses with high switching costs have also recently emerged 
as a potential area of disruption. The increasing use of agents, including by new 
consumer-facing tools such as Muse, may disrupt the business models of some 
consumer firms. Our colleagues in Global Banking & Markets developed a basket of 
“consumer inertia” stocks (GSXUSWCH) where user stickiness is driven more by effort 
required to take action rather than product differentiation. The basket includes 
insurance, telecom, travel, subscriptions, marketplaces, and fintech. The basket has 
lagged a sector-neutral hedge by 5 pp since mid-September.

### Page 13

Enterprise AI adoption remains in early stages but spending has recently 
accelerated, suggesting the impact of AI on corporate earnings should grow 
increasingly visible in coming quarters. The Census Bureau survey suggests that 40% 
of large US firms are using AI in the production of goods and services. Our analysis of Q2 
earnings commentary suggests roughly 50% of S&P 500 companies are using AI in the 
context of productivity, but very few companies have quantified the productivity uplift 
today. However, the revenue run rates of model providers and alternative measures of 
company spending on AI have accelerated during the last several months alongside the 
increasing use of agentic harnesses. 
In contrast with past periods of market exuberance, the equity market today does 
not appear to be pricing expectations of widespread productivity gains. Baskets of 
AI productivity beneficiaries have traded roughly sideways on a relative basis YTD, 
matching the trajectory of near-term forward EPS estimates. Similarly, the measure of 
market-implied long-term growth expectations in Exhibit 22 is only slightly above the 
historical average (for details, see US Equity Views: The next phases of the AI trade).

王筠婷 / 特用化學、半導體週邊

vanessawang@cathayfut.com.tw

弘塑 3131 TT  ( 電子上游–設備 ) 
 ) 
訂單能見度無虞 
投資與評價 
1. 弘塑 2Q26 毛利率僅達 38.9%， 低於我們原先及市場預期的40%水準，
主要受到添鴻產品結構轉差及新廠折舊攤提影響。 
2. 3Q26 受惠於 CoWoS 等機台認列數成長及強勁拉貨需求，營收達
22.21 億元；研究部預估 EPS 為 15.86 元。 
3. FY26 全年出機量預估維持在 220 至 240 台，展望至 FY27/FY28，
FY27 設備出貨量預估將年增 50%，FY28 亦有機會再成長 50%。 
4. 研究部預估 FY26/27 EPS 分別為 60.66 元/96.33 元，維持弘塑買進投
資建議，目標價 2,500 元，以 26x FY27 獲利預估。 
重點更新 
至 FY28 出貨量能將持續成長 
研究部認為弘塑設備出貨展望能見度將能延伸至 FY28。 由於晶圓廠 、OSAT、
記憶體客戶皆對先進封裝設備需求相當迫切，設備交期已長達 10 至 12 個
月，惟受限於短期人力供應與廠房籌備進度， FY26 全年出機量預估維持在 
220 至 240 台。展望至 FY27/FY28，FY27 設備出貨量預估將年增 50%，
FY28 亦有機會再成長 50%。 
添鴻化學品業務將於 1Q27 逐漸看見轉機 
隨 OSAT 客戶於今年 Q2/Q3 進入裝機密集期，新產線仍須經測試與量產爬
坡，相關的化學品需求通常會在設備交機後約 3~4 個月逐步提升，因此短期
營收仍受新廠折舊與稼動率偏低影響，預期添鴻的整體稼動率與毛利率有望
在 1Q27 逐漸擺脫谷底，迎來較為明顯的營運轉機。 
訂單能見度無虞 
展望 2026 年至 2028 年 ， 在各先進封裝平台強勁需求下 ， 設備產能已告全
滿，交期長達 10 至 12 個月，訂單能見度直達 2H27。就產品組合來看，
設備業務高階產品比重將逐漸提升，預估至 FY27 SoIC /CPO 等營收占比將
提升至 10~15%，添鴻化學品也將受惠於 OSAT 客戶逐漸提升拉貨動能，整
體營收與獲利成長動能明確。研究部預估 FY26/27 EPS 分別為 60.66 元
/96.33 元(YoY＋33.5%/+58.8%)。弘塑目前交易於 35/22 倍之 2026/2027 
年獲利預估。研究部維持買進投資建議，目標價 2,500 元，以 26x FY27 獲
利預估。

### Page 2

訪談內容 
3Q26 受惠於機台認列數成長，營收呈現季增 
弘塑 2Q26 營收 17.27 億元 (QoQ +8.2% / YoY +5.9%)，符合我們原先預估的 16.88 億元；毛
利率僅達 38.9% (QoQ +5.1ppts / YoY -1.7ppts)，低於我們原先及市場預期的 40%水準，主要
受到添鴻產品結構轉差及新廠折舊攤提影響 ；EPS 為 11.18 元 (QoQ -30.6% / YoY +58.6%)，
略低於我們原先預期的 11.73 元。 
3Q26 受惠於 CoWoS 等機台認列數成長及強勁拉貨需求，營收呈現季增，營收達 22.21 億元
(QoQ +28.7%/YoY+48.7%) ；研究部預估毛利率 略為季增至 40.1% (QoQ + 1.2ppts / YoY 
-0.5ppts)，產品組合持續改善，預估 EPS 為 15.86 元(QoQ +41.9%/ YoY +52.5%)。 
展望 4Q26， 受惠於先進封裝及記憶體等強勁需求的持續出機認列，研究部預期營收將持續季增，
預估 4Q26 營收將達 24.87 億元(QoQ +12.0%/YoY+15.7%)，毛利率將持穩於 40.0% (QoQ 
-0.1ppts / YoY -2.1ppts)。整體而言，預估 FY26 全年營收達 80.32 億元(YoY+23.3%)，毛利率
達 38.5%(YoY-2.6 ppts)，EPS 為 60.66 元(YoY+33.5%)。

資料來源：弘塑，國泰證期研究部整理 
至 FY28 出貨量能將持續成長 
研究部認為弘塑設備出貨展望能見度將能延伸至 FY28。由於晶圓廠、OSAT、記憶體客戶皆對先
進封裝設備需求相當迫切，設備交期已長達 10 至 12 個月，惟受限於短期人力供應與廠房籌
備進度，FY26 全年出機量預估維持在 220 至 240 台。為突破產能瓶頸，新竹租賃廠房已展開
重新配置，預計於 2026 年底投產，同時公司亦積極評估購置新竹現成廠房。 
展望至 FY27/FY28，FY27 設備出貨量預估將年增 50%，FY28 亦有機會再成長 50%。至 FY27，
OSAT 客戶的設備訂單將較 2026 年呈現倍數成長，正式接棒成為 FY27 推升公司營運成長的最
大主力。至 FY28 及中長期，受惠於先進封裝發展趨勢明確，除出貨量增加外，SoIC、CPO 及
面板級封裝等封裝技術迭代帶動設備 ASP 顯著提升（SoIC 設備較 CoWoS 設備單價增加 30% 
至 50%，面板級封裝達 2.5 至 3 倍以上），外包比例亦有望因自家產能開出而下降，整體營運呈
現量價齊揚。

### Page 3

添鴻化學品業務將於 1Q27 逐漸看見轉機 
隨 OSAT 客戶於今年 Q2/Q3 進入裝機密集期，新產線仍須經測試與量產爬坡，相關的化學品需
求通常會在設備交機後約 3~4 個月逐步提升，因此短期營收仍受新廠折舊與稼動率偏低影響，
預期添鴻的整體稼動率與毛利率有望在 1Q27 逐漸擺脫谷底，迎來較為明顯的營運轉機。 
在設備出機量持續提升 ， 且化學品業務將於FY27 看見較大轉機 ，研究部預估 FY27 營收達 115.49
億元(YoY+43.8%)。利潤率將受惠於產品組合轉好、外包比例降低而提升，預估 FY27 全年毛利
率達 43.0%(YoY+4.5ppts)，全年 EPS 為 96.33 元(YoY+58.8%)。 
訂單能見度無虞 
展望 2026 年至 2028 年，在各先進封裝平台強勁需求下 ， 設備產能已告全滿 ， 交期長達 10 至 
12 個月，訂單能見度直達 2H27。就產品組合來看，設備業務高階產品比重將逐漸提升，預估
至 FY27 SoIC/CPO 等營收占比將提升至 10~15%， 添鴻化學品也將受惠於OSAT 客戶逐漸提升
拉貨動能，整體營收與獲利成長動能明確。 
研究部預估 FY26/27 EPS 分別為 60.66 元/96.33 元(YoY＋33.5%/+58.8%)。弘塑目前交易於
35/22 倍之 2026/2027 年獲利預估 。 研究部維持買進投資建議 ， 目標價2,500 元，以 26x FY27 
獲利預估。

公司簡介 
弘塑集團由四家公司組成： 2013 年合併化學品公司添鴻、 2015 年投資日本公司 T azmo/ 
Apprecia、2018 年合併佳霖、收購太引，並投資 SVXR。

資料來源：弘塑，國泰證期研究部整理

### Page 4

ESG 
弘塑 ESG 報告書依循由全球永續性報告協會(Global Reporting Initiative, GRI) 所發布之永續性報
導準則(GRI Standards)之核心選項編撰，同時參考上市上櫃公司永 續發展實務守則及聯合國永續
發展目標，揭露永續發展相關資訊。報告書中所揭  露的統計數據皆由譜瑞各權責單位彙整提供。
財務績效數據係經資誠聯合會計師事務所依照國際財務報導準則 (International Financial 
Reporting Standards, IFRS)查核簽證之年度合併財務報告。 
除了開發具有競爭力的產品外，弘塑亦以實踐永續發展自期，對商業行為採取最  高專業及道德的
標準，善盡對利害關係人與環境的關心與責任，整合公司資源遵守永續性原則，並持續進行各項改
善。 
經濟面： 
A. 股東權益：公司優點為董事長親自出席股東常會；股東會結束後公開揭露逐項議案之詳細
表決資訊。法規遵循方面，公司於題項評估期間未有不合理或受主管機關處分之情事。(例
如：訴訟、未分派股利、未說明受理小股東提案期間、持股質押比逾 50%、庫藏股買回
或私募股票理由不合理之情形)。 
B. 董事會結構與運作：公司優點為公司未有政府機關、屬同集團之公司或財團法人占董事會
席次達三分之一；揭露審計委員會運作情形；針對功能性委員會進行績效評估，並揭露評
估結果。公司可加強處為法定外功能性委員會之獨立董事未達半數，或未揭露該委員會之
組成、職責與運作情形；董事會之外部績效評估頻率未達標準。獨立董事方面，公司優點
為至少 2 名獨立董事之連續任期不超過 9 年。董事會永續治理方面，公司優點為負責永
續發展或企業社會責任的最高負責人為總經理；當年度永續報告書經董事會通過。董事會
績效與薪酬方面，公司可加強處為未揭露董事薪酬與績效評估結果之連結；企業永續績效
尚未與高階主管及董事薪酬進行連結。董事會多元性方面，公司優點為揭露董事會多元化
政策並揭露落實情形；董事會成員具備不同專業背景。 
C. 行為準則與內控：公司優點為制定反貪腐政策；制定檢舉制度並揭露檢舉情形；訂定內部
規則禁止內部人利用公司內部訊息於有價證券市場獲利 。 公司可加強處為未揭露反競爭或
反托拉斯的相關政策。內控單位與人才方面，公司優點為設置誠信經營負責單位；設置公
司治理主管，並說明職權範圍、業務執行重點及進修情形；內部人員有取得內部稽核相關
證照，例如：內部稽核師。法規遵循方面，評估期間公司未違反重大誠信經營與內控準則
等公司治理相關法規。 
D. 風險及危機管理：公司優點為訂立各種類型的風險回應策略；考量營運中對價值鏈所產生
的環境及社會外部性並揭露相關作為。公司可加強處為未揭露風險管控專責單位；未說明
其風險政策經董事會通過。資訊安全方面，公司優點為設置資安長或資訊安全專責單位；
制定資訊管理政策；制定定期檢視、測試資訊安全機制，並說明檢視成果；提供員工資訊
安全相關教育訓練。公司可加強處為未取得資訊安全相關認證；未揭露個資相關政策。 
E. ESG 創新：公司可加強處為未揭露綠色相關部門設置情形與職責；未揭露綠色/永續採購
之政策或金額 。 此外 ， 產業創新方面 ， 公司優點為對其專利之智慧財產權進行管理與保護，
並揭露量化績效。法規遵循方面，公司近 3 年並未受公平交易委員會懲罰。

### Page 5

社會面： 
A. 人權：公司優點為評估可能遭遇的人權議題，及提出因應措施；揭露人權政策執行結果。
法規遵循方面，評估期間未違反人權相關法規。 
B. 員工溝通與福利：公司優點為定期召開勞資會議供員工參與討論其權利和義務；揭露優於
法規的員工休假福利；訂定具體之職業安全衛生政策且明確揭露實際成果；設有職業安全
衛生委員會負責工安事項；針對工傷事件，揭露統計數據；設定減少工安事件的目標。在
工會爭議與職業災害方面，未有重大職業災害情事。 
C. 人力資本發展：公司優點為制定員工績效評估辦法且定期評比；提供員工能力發展培育計
畫；揭露當年度員工訓練的投入時間或成本。公司可加強處為未透過指標衡量員工訓練成
果。人才留任方面，公司優點為揭露新進員工人數或其占總員工人數之比例，並依照不同
指標(如性別、年齡等)區分；揭露員工離職人數、離職率或流動率，並依照不同指標(如性
別、年齡等)區分；公司可加強處為未提及員工認股權、持股信託等股權分享計畫；未揭
露員工滿意度調查結果。 
D. 多元組成與包容性：公司優點為針對員工組成之多元性擬定策略與目標，並揭露員工組成
結構。公司可加強處為未揭露各階級男女薪酬比；未揭露女性主管人數或佔比；未說明少
數族群雇用情形。 
E. 身心障礙員工雇用方面，當年度足額雇用身心障礙員工或針對未足額情形提出改善措施。 
F. 供應鏈社會面向控管：公司優點為詳細說明人權政策包含項目；依循《 RBA 責任商業聯
盟準則》保障供應商或承攬商之人權。公司可加強處為未說明在新供應商遴選標準中的
ESG 社會面準則；未說明是否具負責任礦物採購管理流程， 或要求供應商簽屬相關衝突礦
產聲明書。 
G. 客戶關係管理：公司優點為公司透過專人或管道即時處理客戶之意見、揭露客訴情形及改
善措施；揭露客戶滿意度數據並設定客戶滿意度目標。法規遵循方面，評估期間並未違反
消費者權益相關爭議或法律訴訟，或已針對違反情形進行說明。 
H. 產品安全：公司優點為自行設立實驗室或委託第三方公正實驗室抽樣產品品質；制定產品
製程持續計畫，確保生產計劃不中斷；取得品質相關認證。公司可加強處為未揭露與產品
品質/服務品質相關之教育訓練時數或費用。 
環境面： 
A.  環境系統與治理：公司優點為回覆 CDP 問卷。公司可加強處為未揭露將綠色概念融入營
運過程的作為。 
B. 空氣管理：公司可加強處為未揭露空氣汙染管理專責單位及相關管理政策、未揭露空氣汙
染排放數據、未揭露量化空氣汙染減量目標。法規遵循方面，評估期間未違反空氣汙染相
關法規或已針對違反情形提出具體改善措施。

### Page 6

C. 能源與氣候變遷：公司優點為說明董事會或管理階層真對氣候變遷議題之監督情形；評估
氣候變遷機會。公司可加強處為未揭露使用具公信力的 1.5 °C 以下及 3 °C 以上溫度情
境評估氣候風險；未揭露與供應鏈或合作夥伴共同推動因應氣候變遷相關策略。溫室氣體
管理方面，公司優點為制定溫室氣體管理政策；揭露至少三年的溫室氣體範疇一與二排放
量，並揭露涵蓋範圍或佔營收比率；制定量化溫室氣體減量目標追蹤績效；通過碳盤查認
證；執行碳足跡制度。公司可加強處為未揭露範疇三排放量。節能管理方面，公司優點為
制定節能管理政策；揭露用電或節能數據；進行能源風險評估。公司可加強處為未揭露節
能量化目標。再生能源管理方面，公司優點為制定再生能源管理政策；公司可加強處為未
揭露再生能源使用量或發電量；未揭露量化再生能源發展目標。 
D. 水管理：公司優點為制定廢水管理政策；公司可加強處為未揭露量化廢汙水減量目標。水
資源管理方面，公司優點為制定水資源管理政策、揭露用水來源；公司可加強處為未揭露
量化用水減量目標。法規遵循方面，評估期間未違反水資源相關法規或已針對違反情形提
出具體改善措施。 
E. 原物料與廢棄物管理：公司優點為揭露廢棄物、資源回收總量。公司可加強處為未揭露原
物料使用量；未揭露量化廢棄物減量目標；未揭露量化有毒／有害廢棄物減量目標。法規
遵循方面 ， 評估期間未違反廢棄物及有害物質相關法規或已針對違反情形提出具體改善措
施。 
F. 供應商及產品生命週期管理：公司優點為制定綠色供應鏈管理政策。公司可加強處為公司
未揭露供應鏈環境管理成效。

## Document 467: 【國泰證期研究部】衛星產業-SpaceX D2C進展更進一步，星艦試射進展順利-20261016

Source PDF: all report pdf/【國泰證期研究部】衛星產業-SpaceX D2C進展更進一步，星艦試射進展順利-20261016.pdf
Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-10-16
Pages: 5
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

1 
產業更新 – 衛星產業 
SpaceX D2C 進展更進一步，星艦試射進展順利 
What’s New –  SpaceX 收購新頻譜，劍指行動通訊市場 
SpaceX於 10/8宣布收購 Grain Management旗下全美 800MHz低頻頻譜，涵蓋最多 14MHz
配對頻譜，交易金額估約 80 億美元。 800MHz 具備廣域覆蓋及室內穿透優勢，可與既有
2GHz頻譜互補，改善偏遠地區通訊品質。儘管仍待 FCC核准，惟主席 Brendan Carr 對新業
者投資持正面態度，預期有利交易推進。 
Trends/Outlook – 衛星、火箭升級短期影響衛星部屬速度 
3Q26 SpaceX共完成 22次 Starlink低軌衛星部署任務，合計發射 578顆衛星，季減 16.8%，
9M26累計部署 2,167顆。單季發射數量下滑主要受任務頻率減少影響，惟單次平均部署 26.3
顆，略高於 2Q26的 25.7顆。此外，SpaceX於 9月成功透過 Starship部署 26顆新一代 V3
衛星，顯示大型運載火箭逐步具備實際衛星部署能力。另一方面，Amazon Leo於 3Q26僅完
成 1次發射任務，部署 29顆衛星，季減 81.3%，主要受運載火箭供應及發射排程限制，後續
須觀察 Vulcan、Ariane 6 及 New Glenn 等新一代火箭投入商業發射的進度。SpaceX另規劃
於路易斯安那州興建全球第四座太空港，預計 2027年動工、2029年首飛，規劃設置逾 12座
發射塔，全面建成後目標每年支援數千次 Starship飛行任務。

Investment Summary 
1. 研究部認為，EchoStar交易解決了 Starlink下一代 D2C的頻譜與容量問題，而近期收購
Grain Management 旗下全美 800MHz低頻頻譜，則進一步補強低頻地面覆蓋資源。有
了自有頻譜資源，搭配未來新一代 D2C衛星酬載之升級，SpaceX 已逐步豐富期從衛星寬
頻業者，走向整合型行動通訊服務供應商的完整資源。 
2. 維持 SpaceX 26/27 年新增 4,000/6,000 顆衛星預期不變，儘管 3Q26 新增數略低於預期，
短期受到新世代衛星、火箭轉換，影響現階段 Falcon 9星鏈任務次數部屬，惟考量下一代 
V3 衛星試射順利，4Q26有望啟動部屬，單次 Starlink 低軌衛星發射數量上限將由 29 顆
大幅上升至  60 顆，將有益於未來衛星成長在加速 ，故持續看好天上衛星供應鏈如華通
(2313 TT)、昇達科(3491 TT)、燿華(2367 TT)後市發展。

投資 顧問

2 
SpaceX 收購新頻譜，劍指行動通訊市場 
SpaceX於 10/8宣布與數位基礎建設投資公司 Grain Management達成協議，擬收購美國全國性
800MHz低頻頻譜牌照組合，涵蓋最多 14MHz配對頻譜，市場預估交易金額約 80億美元。由於
800MHz具備較佳的訊號穿透能力及廣域覆蓋優勢，可與既有 2GHz中頻頻譜形成互補，提升室內
及偏遠地區通訊品質。該收購案目前雖仍需要 FCC進行批准，不過 FCC主席 Brendan Carr目前已
表態，認為更多投資與服務業者加入，將有助於推動新一代連線技術發展，因此預期此次收購將順
利完成，未來將加速 Starlink Mobile整合衛星與地面行動網路，強化 D2D服務覆蓋能力，並逐步
降低對傳統電信商的依賴，為 SpaceX進軍美國行動通訊市場奠定基礎。 
SpaceX於 25年進行第一次的頻譜收購，向美國衛星及電信業者 EchoStar收購旗下無線通訊頻譜
使用權及相關資產，主要獲得 AWS-4 使用權之 2GHz頻譜，初步奠定了 SpaceX在行動衛星通訊
的基礎，且逐步掌握自主頻譜資源。然衛星通訊連線存在的痛點之一，便是經常因建築物、遮蔽物
等造成訊號傳遞出現障礙，因此過去 Starlink用戶終端天線皆需要安裝在水平面 15 度以上、天空
完全無遮蔽的屋頂或陽台上。而在新取得的低頻頻譜後，SpaceX 將具備傳輸距離遠、可穿透建築
物及樹木等障礙物的優勢，有助改善衛星行動通訊的室內訊號覆蓋能力，降低對傳統基地台的依賴。
另外目前大多數行動手機均支援 800MHz頻段，因此未來消費者若使用 Starlink Mobile服務，無
須另外購買專用衛星電話。 
圖一：Starlink Mobile 服務應用示意圖                                               圖二：D2C 衛星酬載 
資料來源：SpaceX、國泰證期研究部整理 
研究部認為，EchoStar交易解決了 Starlink下一代 D2C的頻譜與容量問題，而近期的收購則進一
步補強低頻地面覆蓋資源。有了自有頻譜資源，搭配未來新一代 D2C衛星酬載之升級，SpaceX 已
逐步豐富期從衛星寬頻業者，走向整合型行動通訊服務供應商的完整資源。

### Page 3

投資 顧問 
產業報告 
 2026/10/16

3 
新世代衛星、火箭升級短期影響衛星部屬速度 
3Q26 SpaceX共計完成 22次 Starlink低軌衛星部署任務，合計發射 598顆衛星，包含次軌道測試
衛星 20顆，季減 11.1%，年初累計至 9M26共部署 2,167顆。其中 7、8、9月分別部署 210、
261及 107顆，9月發射數量明顯下降，推測因 Starship試飛任務導致 Falcon 9 星鏈任務減少影
響，單季任務數由 2Q26的 27次降至 22次。然 3Q26單次任務平均部署衛星顆數約為 26.3顆，
略高於 2Q26的 25.7顆，顯示整體發射數量下滑主要來自任務頻率減少，而非單次載運數量下降。
此外，SpaceX於 9月 28日成功透過 Starship Flight 14 將 26顆新一代 Starlink V3 衛星送入軌
道，為 Starship首次完成正式衛星入軌部署。隨著下一代衛星導入更大型天線與高功率通訊酬載，
單顆衛星重量及通訊容量同步提升，目前短期可能限制 Falcon 9單次部署數量，但長期隨 Starship
發射能力逐步成熟，有望加速高容量 Starlink V3衛星部署，帶動整體星系寬頻容量持續擴張。 
此外，另一位衛星運營商 Amazon Leo 3Q26僅完成 1次低軌衛星發射任務，部署 29顆衛星，數
量大幅季減 81.3%，年初至今共部署 216顆衛星。3Q26發射任務於 7/2由 ULA Atlas V 火箭執
行，其後 8、9月均無新增衛星部署，顯示 Amazon Leo 衛星部署進度仍受到運載火箭供應及發射
排程限制。我們認為，Amazon Leo目前主要瓶頸在於發射運能，而非衛星製造能力，後續須觀察
包含 ULA Vulcan、Arianespace Ariane 6及 Blue Origin New Glenn 等新一代火箭逐步投入商業
發射，是否能夠帶動 Amazon Leo發射進程提升。 
圖三：Starbase Louisiana 示意圖 
資料來源：interstellar gateway、國泰證期研究部整理 
SpaceX 於今年 8 月拍板定案，將於美國路易斯安那州南部 Vermilion Parish興建全球第四座太空
發射基地（Starbase Louisiana） ，基地位於墨西哥灣沿岸，占地約12.5萬英畝（約 5.1萬公頃） ，
面積超過紐約曼哈頓 8倍，預計 2027年正式動工，並於 2029年執行首次 Starship發射任務。基
地規劃設置超過 12座發射塔，全面建成後目標每年支援數千次 Starship飛行任務。研究部預期，
隨著 Starship逐步實現高頻率發射與火箭重複使用，新增發射基地將有助於突破既有太空港的發射
排程及運能限制，進一步加速 Starlink下一代 V3衛星部署，並提升 SpaceX大規模建置低軌衛星
的能力。
